(Ch 139.)** **A conservator I cannot pay is a conservator I have to keep asking, and asking her to be useful is asking her to be cheap, and I have been doing the second thing since the twenty-seventh of January and calling it the first, and she has let me do it, and the reason she has let me do it is that she would rather be useful than be forty marks in front of a man who cannot pay her, and that was a kindness and I have taken it for eight months and called it a trade.** And the standing arrangement that replaces it: the party will keep asking and will not ask her to be useful, and a conservator of no office in Morrow Quay is to be found on a Friday morning at about ten in a room open to anybody, **and the party did not arrange that and would not dare to.**
- **PAID — THE REEVE SPENDS AGAIN AND THE PRICE LANDS ON SOMEBODY WHO IS NOT THE YARD, AND NEITHER MAN GETS WHAT HE WANTS. (Ch 140, Wednesday the twenty-sixth.)** The mole's footing from the third seam to the twelfth to be examined on the second of August, the third lay's moorings closed for the four days of it, **entered in an office whose repair account has been empty since the middle of February, with the reason on the face of it: an office whose instruments nobody contests can enter an instrument with nothing behind it, and that is worse than no instrument.** A man of the third lay says four days out loud in a market in forty people, with the tide right on two of them and wrong on the others, and says the rest in the order that matters and does not ask for anything. **A woman of no office says the thing none of them can say: four days with a lay shut is four days with a hull's bottom out of the water and nobody on it.** The plate is referred to **twice in the whole volume, the second time in thirteen words, and reached in neither**: *it is not and I will not tell you what it is about.* **THE MECHANISM REMAINS A CHANGE OF POLITICS IN AN OFFICE AND IT HAS NOW PRODUCED A SECOND THING IN THAT OFFICE'S BOOK AFTER AN END OF THE FIRST OF SEPTEMBER ON A MOORING: four days of a lay, closed, for an examination with no money behind it.**
- **PAID — THE FORWARD PULL. (Ch 140, at about half past six.)** **A man of the third lay says a boat's name out loud in a street: there is a working boat with people in her lying off the outer marks, the tide gang has walked past her every day since the ninth of July and nobody has told them anything, her name is the *Hearthwake*, and a man of a boat of his says she is one of the nine. THE YARD WRITES IT DOWN WHERE ANYBODY CAN CHECK IT, WHICH IS A PAGE IN A BOOK IN A SHED ON A HILL, AND DOES NOT GO AND LOOK, AND SENDS NOBODY, AND ASKS THAT MAN NO QUESTION ABOUT HER, AND SAYS OUT LOUD THE FOURTH THING IT ENTERED ON ITS OWN ACCOUNT ON THE FIFTH OF JULY, WORD FOR WORD: the outer marks stand where the writ of the first of May says they are, the bar lies four hundred yards off them, and this party is not going past them this season and has not tried and is not going to. AND THAT IS NOT NOTHING AND IT IS NOT ENOUGH AND BOTH OF THOSE ARE ON THE PAGE.**

### Part

- **PART — The condition. (Ch 131, the eighth; Ch 134, the thirteenth; Ch 137, the twentieth, NAMED.)** **It is on the page in this batch four times and the fourth carries a name.** The eighth of July, in the second column, a man could not for about four minutes tell which of his two hands was holding the line. The thirteenth, in the return, the same fact as one of four second-column lines. The eighteenth, nineteenth and twentieth, in the water and in a boat, a bad day on all three. **The twentieth, the word.** **The bare fact does not vanish: it is carried four times in this batch, with a reason against each, and with the count of four in the first column in another person's hand, and the fourth of the four above the named line has no name and is not struck out and a request to strike it out is refused.** The one naming Volume 03 is allowed is **spent, and is not available to any later movement of this volume or to any other.**
- **PART — Bevin Rook. (Named only; not in the batch.)** **HE IS IN NO CHAPTER OF IT.** Not written to, not visited, nobody asked what he said to them. **The three nots of the eighth of June are recited in four chapters and none is broken.** Forty marks standing whole; a wage running into an envelope; nine envelopes on a bench and not one of them anywhere. **NOT PAID, NOT REPAIRED, NOT REDUCED. MOVEMENT 5 MAY NOT PAY IT OFF EITHER.**
- **PART — Sella Brigg's warp. (Ch 131, the seventh; Ch 140, the twenty-sixth.)** **A crew of Ashtide takes about four hundred feet of her own warp off the bottom of this harbor on FRIDAY the twenty-eighth of July for twenty-six marks and a day — *the weekday is corrected here in place in Batch 0005's own pass: Chapters 138, 139 and 140 all establish that the twenty-sixth of July is a Wednesday, and Chapter 131 has the woman herself say in her own mouth that he will not do it on a Tuesday tide. The chapters are right and this line was wrong, and the taking of the warp is in Chapter 141* — and this yard told her to go to him on the seventh and is not going in the way of it, and on the seventh she said out loud in a market that the yard's own sheet is not on that wall any more and that there is a man from Ashtide over the top of it with four marks at the head of it, and she said it is not to be cruel.** Nothing is raised and nothing is sold by this yard and the eight shillings and tenpence to each of two women stand undiminished and unpaid.
- **PART — Edric Pringle's day and her two debts. (Ch 133, Ch 136, Ch 137.)** **She hires a day at this yard's own rate, pays twelve of seventeen at the mole, gets half of it, comes up the hill to say the cause out loud, checks this yard's own book on Thursday the twenty-third for the two-debts rule and comes up again to say it is right, and is owed eight shillings and tenpence and does not ask.** **THE TWO DEBTS ARE NEVER IN ONE SENTENCE AND THE TWO WOMEN ARE NEVER MERGED, AND ONE OF THEM IS A HIRER OF THIS YARD'S DAY AND THE OTHER IS TAKING HER WARP TO A MAN WITH A COMPRESSOR, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH PUTS THEM TOGETHER.**
- **PART — The third column. (Two entries in this batch: the tenth of July at about two hours and the twenty-second at about three hours, with the two earlier figures of the previous batches standing. Seven in all since the second of May.)** Each carries two times at the head of it and a lateness against it. **The party has entered the two figures and has entered that the two figures are not a trend and is not going to draw anything out of them.** The head of the column has two times on it that a boy of twenty-two put there on the second of May, and a line under the head in the holder's own left hand saying the lead plug is not where that goes and is not going in a second column either.
- **PART — The harbor's reader. (Ch 133, the eleventh.)** **She prices a hand that will last a week at four marks a week, says the reader thing once in a different sentence that means the same thing, says a thing said three times in a shed is a complaint and she does not make complaints, and says she is not sorry they put the boy on the stone on the fifteenth of May. It is in the first column because she said it in a shed. Nobody in this harbor is hiring at four marks a week to read a slate back.**

### Carried

- **CARRIED — The coast road.** Not resolved, not explained, not named a network by anybody, **not connected to the join and not connected to anything under this town, and the yard refuses the connection on the page twice in this batch.** Its figures are printed in a market and going to press in a city. **The pilot out of a storm belt is still coming in the autumn for nothing, with no instrument in this harbor that can call him and none that can stop him, NO ENTRY ABOUT WAITING FOR HIM IS ON THE PAGE ANYWHERE, AND THE YARD DID NOT GO AND LOOK THIS MONTH.**
- **CARRIED — The glass storm.** Not used. Two written occurrences, two second-column lines in two hands on two pages that are not on top of each other, **no cause in any volume, nothing in either that knows anything.**
- **CARRIED — The delayed note.** Eleven logged occasions and two empty answers, **neither moved by anything in this batch and neither moved by a measurement. No occasion is entered. The delay is a hand's width of a second and has not moved and was not taken.**
- **CARRIED — The bell.** Fouled, no tongue on the frame, two parts of a chain in a lead plug where the tongue was, a frame cut through the casting on one side. **THE GOING OUT OF THE SECOND OF MAY IS DONE AND SPENT AND IS NOT REPEATED, NO SECOND EIGHT MARKS IS PAID, NO BOAT GOES OFF THE SOUTH END IN THIS BATCH, AND THE STANDING SENTENCE SAID IN A STREET ON THE THIRD OF MAY STANDS UNREVERSED AND IS RECITED IN FOUR CHAPTERS.** **ITS NOTATION IS IN A NAVAL FILE AND IS NOT ON A WALL IN THIS HARBOR AND CANNOT BE TAKEN DOWN.** Eight marks and a day of a boat owing since the twenty-third of May, unpaid, unargued, not paid.
- **CARRIED — The lead plug.** **HE WAS NOT ASKED AGAIN IN THIS BATCH AND WAS NOT IN IT. No digit is printed in any chapter of this batch. The chest was not opened and was not brought down the hill. The undertaking of the second of July binds the yard for the rest of the volume.**
- **CARRIED — Custody.** Three items, three numbers, digits not quoted, none returns, none is asked for, the municipal claim on the third stands and the yard will not make a request. **No paper states what a municipal claim does to a presumed Crown material and no clerk answered it.**
- **CARRIED — The concealment.** Hidden. The Oren mark is in no record, in no office, in no book and not in Kest's chart notation. **Nobody asked the boy and nobody asked the conservator, and the boy is not in the yard and is not to be written to.**
- **CARRIED — Neave Tarrant.** Nine winters, out since the twenty-eighth of February, not found, one line in the yard's book, and **this yard has not gone and has asked nobody anything**, and the line is recited in the read-outs.
- **CARRIED — The fifth-seam plate.** Still in eleven feet, seen and not measured and not touched. **Referred to twice in the volume, once in June in a room by a man who had been told what a thing costs, and on the twenty-sixth of July on the mole's steps in thirteen words, when the holder asked whether the morning's entry was about it and the reeve said it is not and would not say what it is about. The plan is not reopened and no chapter of this batch reached for it.** The mechanism remains **a change of politics in an office** and it has now produced two things in that office's book.
- **CARRIED — Labor, whole.** **Nothing in a bag, thirty-one on the note, nothing in a tin. Fourteen lines and fifty-six marks in the wage column with the fifth of March the oldest, nine envelopes on a bench and not one of them anywhere. Twelve marks in and sixteen out in twenty days and no office in this harbor that has paid this yard a mark since the eighth of May and none that is going to.** Forty marks of claim standing whole. Forty marks to a conservator with no office, first after food, nine months by her own figure and by her own hand, not one mark paid. Eight shillings and tenpence to each of two women, in no sentence together. Nineteen winters, in a naval file and in a letter and in a list. Eight marks and a day of a boat to a bellwright. A compressor's gland on the note at five marks, unpaid, useless, because the thing after the gland is the hose. No delivery hose and a crew of Ashtide at the outer end of the second lay with a compressor and forty fathoms of her own. Two pots of air bought this month at two marks apiece and they are the whole of the air in this harbor. The ninth seam out with an end entered for the first of September. **A third lay closed four days in August by an office with an empty repair account.** The north berth out. The third lay full. The tide gang four men short. A cistern charged four times in this batch and twelve times on that office's page at no figure, holding since the nineteenth of June, **and one rota day deliberately not walked on the seventeenth with the reason on the page in the yard's own hand, which is that the gate takes four hours with three men and the same three men were on the second seam from seven until about two.** The fire party's hose dead. The light not on. The repair account empty. No boat of this office's own since the twenty-eighth of April. The *Kittiwake* on the hard. The hard at Ashtide. The *Sea Wren* at the pile. Eleven copies, a list of eleven on a wall, a printed sheet over it, **and now a Crown attachment over that, and a printer in a city told in advance.** A man of the third lay, nine winters, not found, and this yard has not gone. **A hand that will last a week at four marks a week, which is a wage and is not a fee and is in no column in this book.** Twenty marks of unsold iron and four hundred and forty pounds of old iron in a heap that may not be sold, with a bell-metal gun-port in it that was not looked at in this batch. Two objects of nobody's in the open air on a mole's crown, one tarpaulin with a corner off it since the seventeenth of June, **and the whole of this month's business nine feet underneath them.**

### Ruled

- **RULED — THE ONE NAMING OF THE CONDITION WAS SPENT AND IS SPENT, IN CHAPTER 137, ON THURSDAY THE TWENTIETH OF JULY, IN THE YARD'S OWN BOOK, IN THE HOLDER'S OWN LEFT HAND, IN THE SECOND COLUMN, WITH A REASON AGAINST THE LINE. IT IS NOT AVAILABLE AGAIN IN THIS VOLUME OR ANY OTHER, AND THE NEXT BATCH MUST NOT PUT THE WORD IN.**
- **RULED — THE BELLWRIGHT WAS NOT ASKED AGAIN.** **HE IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH. HE IS NOT IN A SHED, NOT IN A BOAT, NOT IN A MARKET, NOT ON A MOLE AND NOT IN A ROOM, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH PRINTS A DIGIT OF WHAT HE SAID ON THE SECOND OF JULY, AND THE CHEST WAS NOT OPENED AND WAS NOT BROUGHT DOWN THE HILL. This is not a virtue and is not put forward as one: he was not asked because a yard that has undertaken not to ask does not ask, and because the work did not need him.**
- **RULED — THERE ARE FOUR RETURNS AND THERE IS NO FIFTH, and the fourth is the fourteenth of July, on the day, out of a finding of Monday the tenth.** **`outline/volume-03.md` lock L5 enumerates three and says they are the only three. THE PROMPT THAT AUTHORISED THIS BATCH QUALIFIED THAT IN ITS OWN FIRST LINE, AND THE CHAPTERS OBEY THE QUALIFIER AND NOT THE BARE COUNT: there is no fourth and a chapter may not produce one out of a day that produced nothing, AND THE TENTH OF JULY PRODUCED A FINDING OF WORKED GLASS, WHOLE OR IN PART, ON GROUND NO PARTY CLAIMS, CAUGHT BY THE FIRST LIMB OF THE CLAUSE BY ITSELF. THE CLAUSE'S OWN WORDS REQUIRE IT AND THE COUNTING RULE GIVES THE FOURTEENTH. This is recorded, not argued, and a Volume 03 audit should rule whether the lock's enumeration is superseded by the chapters.**
- **RULED — Captain Holt's posting after her defeat is NOT decided and is explicitly still open, and no chapter of this volume may name a posting before the defeat that follows it.** **What Chapters 130 and 140 foreclose on the page, in the yard's own hand, is what a posting may not be: no posting, appointment, commission, transport or instruction out of any city has put anybody past the harbor line, the outer marks and the bar of four hundred yards off them stand where the writ says they are and are not nearer, and the party is not going past them this season and has not tried and is not going to.**
- **RULED — the channel by which a disgraced officer reaches a Morrow Quay reeve is THE WRIT, ruled on the page on Wednesday the fifth of July in Chapter 130, recited in Chapter 140 and applied there when a man gave it a boat's name.** **A man in a coat, of any office, arrives as a stranger with a paper about the second season; the yard will read it, will answer it on the day it comes, and will not treat him as a friend or an enemy. No second channel has been invented and none may be.**
- **RULED — the party's own decision of the fifth of July, that it is not going to be a party to the naming of a road and is not going to put a name on anything it cannot check, held all month and is applied for the first time against a thing it wants.**

### Open by design

- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what the glass in the crevice and the glass in the packing and the glass in the tallow of a lead carry.** **A conservator of no office has said in a market and in a shed that she cannot read it, that the trade that can costs six marks and two days, that none of the three of them has six marks, and that the party will not pay anybody to read it and could not if it would. NO READING OCCURS, NO ECHO IS ENTERED, AND NO CHAPTER CERTIFIES ANYTHING A THING CARRIES. If a later volume reads one, the six marks and two days and the yard's inability to pay are both on the page.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the marked face.** Unidentified, unexplained, not allowed to settle anything, **and not moved: the shard of the tenth of June is on the mole's crown under a tarpaulin with the lump beside it, nine feet above everything in this batch, and it has not been moved and is not going to be moved for the sake of a chapter.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the three places with no joint.** The weir is on this coast. The village is not on this coast and is not on any chart anybody in this harbor has seen. The sand and shell lay is on this coast and is not on any chart in this harbor. **The party has entered the three and has entered that it does not know why the three are there and is not going to guess and is not going to look for them this season.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the figure on the bar of gravel.** A stranger's number against a stranger's number, going to press in a city, and a yard that cannot answer it and has made a rule it will not break. **The figure is Harl Dace's, forty-eight, of the *Grey Widow*, who has a slate and no wheel of his own in this harbor, and the man of fifty-one with the bad wheel is a different man and the two bad wheels are two men and were not in one sentence in this batch.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the first echo's witness.** Unnamed, unlocated, uninterviewed, not in this batch.

### What Movement 5 inherits, in plain terms

**Movement 5 is the volume's climax: the *Vigil* moves on the crew while Calder Marr is trying to raise the *Hearthwake*, he saves the vessel's living crew and loses the chance to carry her full bell-archive to the surface, and the two are the same choice and neither cancels the other. Captain Holt's defeat is in it and her posting after it must be decided on the page with a date. It is owed six things.**

1. **THE ONE NAMING IS GONE. IT WAS SPENT IN CHAPTER 137 AND NOTHING SPENDS IT AGAIN.** The condition is on the page five times in five months and one of the five carries a word, and the four above it have not been struck out and a request to strike one out is refused, and the word is on the page forever and cannot be taken off it.
2. **THE BELLWRIGHT IS SPENT AS A QUESTION. HE WAS NOT ASKED IN MOVEMENT 4 AND HIS UNDERTAKING BINDS THE YARD FOR THE REST OF THE VOLUME.** No digit, no shed, no room, no chest.
3. **THE FAULTREADER STAGE IS TAKEN AND IT IS SPENT. HE READ ONE SEAM AND GOT IT WRONG IN A MARKET AND WAS DISPROVED BY FOULING, AND IT IDENTIFIED DIFFERENCE AND NOT TRUTH, AND NO SEAM MAY SETTLE AN ARGUMENT, PROVE A MOTIVE, CONVICT ANYBODY OR TELL HIM WHAT HAPPENED.** Whether he ever reads another one, and whether a second one is better, is Movement 5's own decision and is not owed by this batch.
4. **MARA VENN IS NOT ON THE CREW AND IS NOT IN THE REGISTRY'S EYES AND HER BREAK WITH THE REGISTRY IS VOLUME 4 AND IS A DIFFERENT EVENT AND NO CHAPTER MAY FUSE THE TWO.** She is in this harbor and is to be found on a Friday morning in a room open to anybody. She is owed forty marks, first after food, unpaid since the fifteenth of October, and **she has now said out loud that she is not going to be useful again and the party has entered that it will keep asking her and will not ask her to be useful. If a chapter in the climax needs a conservator, THAT ARRANGEMENT IS THE PRICE OF HER BEING THERE AND NOT A CONVENIENCE.**
5. **THE PUBLIC THING IS DONE AND CANNOT BE UNDONE. The yard's own fourth return is on a market wall with no price on it, a printer in a city has been told in advance, the rival is going to print a better sheet on Monday and says so, and a Crown attachment with a place, an hour, a keeper and limits in it is the only paper in this harbor that any office in any city is obliged to look at.** The party's advantage of a map nobody knew about is gone and it is gone because the party published it on purpose.
6. **THE BOAT IS NAMED AND HAS NOT BEEN LOOKED AT. *Hearthwake*, working, with people in her, off the outer marks since before the ninth of July, one of the nine, the tide gang walking past her daily and told nothing, named in a market in this harbor on the twenty-sixth of July, written into a shed book, and the party has said out loud in a market that the outer marks stand where the writ says they are and it is not going past them this season and has not tried and is not going to. THE WAVE'S UNSPENT ARGUMENT IS THEREFORE ON THE TABLE: the volume's chapter has to be a chapter about what happens when a working crew is on a boat somebody else is working, and the outer marks and the writ's first of March and a Crown attachment on a market wall are three dated things a Movement 5 writer can lean on.** A boat with a bell-archive in her is worth more to her own people than anything else in the water, and the people in her are worth more than the archive to the people who own her, and both of those are true and neither cancels the other.
7. **ALSO CARRIED FORWARD AND NOT SPENT:** the autumn visit of a man of fifty-four with a different figure for the bar and no instrument to call him; Sella Brigg's about four hundred feet of warp and a crew of Ashtide who take it on the twenty-eighth of July; a girl of twenty-two's June and forty marks of claim and a third column the yard intends to keep and cannot yet fill and has now priced at four marks a week; the ninth seam's end of the first of September and what an office with uncontested instruments does next, which it has now done twice; a third lay that has just lost four days of the best of its season to an examination with no money behind it; the first paper out of a city and the answer to it and the second one that will come; and **Captain Holt's posting, which is still open and must be decided on the page with a date, with the channel already ruled and being the writ.**

---

## Volume 03, Batch 0005 (Chapters 141–150) — as written

**Movement 5 is written, and it is the volume's climax and its resolution. Twenty-nine thousand six hundred and eighty-eight words across ten chapters, Thursday the twenty-seventh of July to Tuesday the fifteenth of August 2026. NO CARD FILE EXISTED FOR THIS BATCH AND NONE WAS INVENTED; the shape was worked out of `outline/volume-03.md` and the four state files Batch 0004 had just written.** Every thread below is marked PAID, PART, CARRIED, RULED or OPEN BY DESIGN.

### Paid

- **PAID — THE CLIMAX, AND IT WAS A CHOICE MADE WITH HIS HANDS. (Ch 147, Tuesday the eighth of August, a working day.)** The party goes past the outer marks on its own account with no instrument of any office, having entered the breaking of its own published sentence and the whole of the cost in the first column **in advance, on the Monday night, in the holder's own left hand.** Three calls down, seventeen minutes each, the six read aloud in order at the boat. A case of five bells comes up through a seized grating on a bight of the *Sallow Reach's* warp and stands on end in three feet of water with the lid against the *Hearthwake's* own rail. **At about twenty past three the *Vigil's* grapnel comes tight, she goes over another three foot, and a boy of seventeen goes off her foredeck into seven fathoms; he shouted; and for about a second and a half Calder Marr could not tell whether the shout came from the water or from the deck; and HE LET GO OF THE LINE, and the case went down straight and the grating lid was the last of it.** Nineteen minutes in the water, the woman holding the boy on from her own doorway with a boathook, the *Sallow Reach* taking him off her own bow, the fourth man off on his own account with a line the boy of seventeen had put round his own waist before he went over the rail. **Nessa Pike never said the word and the reason is on the page, and the reason includes what she would have done at twenty-five.**
- **PAID — THE COST, AND IT IS PAID BY SOMEBODY WHO IS NOT THE YARD. THE BELL-ARCHIVE IS LOST.** A community's only local copy of its own account of itself, in five bells, in about five and a half fathoms under the after hatch of a vessel claimed whole by an office in a city, off a mole nobody in this harbor may go past on a writ. **The four people it belongs to are alive and have no boat, no wages and about nine marks between them. THE OWNERS ARE NOT MISSING AND CANNOT BE TOLD IN A MARKET, AND WERE NOT: the page of the fifteenth of August says where the bells are and does not pretend anybody is coming for them. A crate half out of a hatch in a boat is not a raising, the reporting clause does not reach that ground, and there is no fifth return.**
- **PAID — BOTH OF THOSE ARE TRUE AND NEITHER CANCELS THE OTHER, SAYED THREE TIMES, BY THREE DIFFERENT PEOPLE, AND NESSA VALE OF THE SILL SAYS IT FIRST.** **First by Nessa Vale, in the open air on the mole's steps on the tenth, in the middle of eleven minutes of testimony, and a quarter of an hour before the holder says it in the same scene — where he says it again and then yields the first saying to her in front of forty people, refusing to go round the side of a limit four people wrote; and last by Elias Voke, a herring skipper of fifty-one with nineteen winters in a naval file because a reeve sold a chart for them, in a market on the thirteenth, who said it once, would not say it twice, and made Calder Marr read the last sentence back twice.** It is the outline's sentence and it is the sentence the volume closes on, and it is entered in the first column on the thirteenth. **The yard's own book holds it three times — out of a room in April, out of a market in July, and out of the market on the thirteenth — and the holder has entered that he did not write down the one said on the mole on the tenth.**
- **PAID — THE OUTER-MARKS SENTENCE IS BROKEN AND THE BREAKING IS ON THE PAGE IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND AND IN ADVANCE.** Monday the seventh of August, nine at night: that the party is going, that the sentence of the fifth and twenty-sixth of July is broken and that this party broke it, and the cost, being the air, the *Sallow Reach* part paid, the wage of the same Tuesday, and **the sentence itself, which cannot be said again**, with the reason — a yard that has said a thing in a market and has not done it is a yard whose saying is worth nothing in any figure it puts on any wall in any year after this one. **A WAGE OF FOUR MARKS WAS NOT PAID ON TUESDAY THE FIFTEENTH OF AUGUST AND THE ENVELOPE WAS NOT MADE, and three marks is owing to a man of Ashtide, and the wage column stops at sixteen lines and sixty-four marks with the fifth of March still the oldest.**
- **PAID — THE *VIGIL* MOVES ON A WORKING BOAT OVER A MARKET WALL, AND IS DEFEATED WITHOUT A COURT.** Her notice of the twenty-eighth of July is nailed over a return made under a party's own writ; her notice of the fourth of August says no assistance will be rendered and that every part of the vessel and her contents is Crown material, and the seal on it is ink and off by a sixteenth of an inch. She grounds the vessel with her own grapnel on the second of August. She is emptied in the open by a claim not sustainable in the form it was brought. **She does not appear in a room, on a mole, or in this harbor, and is described from a paper.**
- **PAID — CAPTAIN HOLT'S POSTING, DECIDED ON THE PAGE WITH A DATE, VIA THE WRIT.** Read in the hearing room on Saturday the twelfth of August by a man in a coat of an office in a city whose name the clerk wrote down and this yard has not repeated: **the *Vigil* is not to be employed upon this coast; Captain Veyra Holt is relieved of that vessel and of the command of this coast from the fifteenth of August instant and is posted from the first of September instant to the making up of the coast's wreck lists, in a city, at a table, with every vessel reported, claimed, entered and closed upon this coast for the year.** **IT IS NOT A COMMAND IN THIS HARBOR, IT IS NOT AN INSPECTION OF OR OVER THE HARBOR AUTHORITY, IT IS NOT A COMMISSION, AND IT PUTS NO PERSON, NO BOAT AND NO LEAD PAST THE OUTER MARKS OR PAST THE IMPERIAL LINE, AND THE YARD CHECKED IT AGAINST ITS OWN ENTRY OF THE FIFTH OF JULY AND ENTERED THAT THE CHECK HOLDS.** **The second season of the writ is not granted, not refused and not dealt with by that paper; the writ runs to the first of March; its ground is not widened; its four names are four.**
- **PAID — THE CHANNEL IS THE WRIT AND IT WAS APPLIED, AND IT WAS THE REASON THE MAN WAS LET UP THE STAIRS.** Ruled on the fifth of July, recited on the twenty-sixth, applied on the twelfth of August: a paper about the second season comes up the hill, the yard reads it, answers it on the day it comes, and does not treat the man who brings it as a friend or as an enemy. **No second channel was invented and none may be.**
- **PAID — THE RESCUED CREW TESTIFY IN THEIR OWN WORDS, ON THEIR OWN TERMS, WITH A REAL LIMIT ON IT AND THE LIMIT ENTERED.** **A bell-archive is not a thing that is read out; it is a thing that is struck, in a hall, in front of the people it is about, and there is nobody in this town it is about.** Four people of the Sill wrote that themselves on a mole's steps in about forty people's hearing on the tenth of August and signed it, and it is a real limit: nothing in a shed, nothing into a book that goes out of this harbor, nothing to a man with a seal, not one word about who took their vessel off them, and no name to any office in any city. **SOMEBODY BEARS IT: the four bear it, and the holder bears it, and the entry says in the yard's own hand and with a date that he wanted it lifted, could not lift it, did not ask twice, and is not going to.**
- **PAID — THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION IS ASKED IN A MARKET AND IS NOT ANSWERED, AND THE REFUSAL IS ON THE PAGE IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND.** Sunday the thirteenth of August, in about forty people's hearing on the boards: **WHO CHANGED THE RULE.** He does not know, says that a man who has never heard the question says *what rule* and a man who has heard it says *I do not know*, says he has thought about it for five days and is not going to go round the side of a limit four people put on their own account, and says he cannot give a name because he does not know what the rule was called or what it changed from. **The not-answering is entered with the reason against it, and the reason is this yard's decision of the fifth of July not to put a name on a thing it cannot check. THE NINE HARBORS ARE NOT NAMED AS A COMPACT, THE QUIETENING IS NOT NAMED, THE OFFICE OF RETURNING MEMORY IS NOT NAMED, AND NO OFFICE IN VEVR IS SHOWN THE DOCUMENT THAT WOULD ANSWER IT.**
- **PAID — THE FINAL CHAPTER IMAGE, ALL OF IT, IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND.** **A page goes up on a market wall UNDER A NAVAL PAPER on Tuesday the fifteenth of August at about eleven in the morning, in a market, in daylight, on the same nails, on the outside of whatever was there** — and the *Vigil's* notice of the fourth of August is over it, over the *Sallow Reach's* printed sheet, over the sheet of the seventh of June, over the yard's tenth thing, and the yard's own fourth return of the twenty-second of July has been under four papers since the twenty-eighth of July and can still be read from the side of it in the right wind. **The page carries THE PLACE OF EVERY COPY OF IT (about sixty boats, one copy in a city going to press, four of the eleven copies of the older sheet not reached and not going to be); THE NINE PLACES AS THIS YARD IS ABLE TO STATE THEM, WITH A DATE AGAINST EACH, and it does not call them a chain and does not call them a compact and THE WORD *HARBORS* IS NOT ON IT ANYWHERE; and THE TWO COLUMNS, nine lines of the first and five of the second, with a reason against every line of the second, in full, none shortened.** **The chandler's note is not re-written and is SAID OUT LOUD at the head of the dock in nine people's hearing, with its three counts of weeks standing at twenty-two, thirteen and eleven and the refusal of the thirty-first of March unreversed and the reason that re-writing a note with nothing in the bag is not a payment.** **In about four years a bell is going to be out in a boat and the whole of the craft is the going out and it stands unreversed; the boy who used to read the day back is not on the stone; there is a pencil in that yard tonight and it is in somebody else's hand; and the delay is a hand's width of a second and nobody has said why.**
- **PAID — A BOY WHO CANNOT DO THE THING HE WAS KEPT FOR, IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND, WITH A DATE, AND NOT A LESSON LEARNED BY ANYBODY.** First column, Tuesday the first of August, the holder's own left hand: the thing is reading a position back into a slate for a man in a boat; he did it for two hundred days; on the fourth of May he read four figures and got all four right and on the eleventh he read two and got one wrong; he was put off the stone on the fifteenth of May on a date the party named before anybody asked; **he has not done it since and is not going to and cannot, and the party has entered that he cannot, has not asked him, is not going to, and is not going to hire a boy to do it and call that the same thing, and a column in that book is not a wage.** **The head of the third column keeps his hand and a request to scrape it out is refused. The forty marks stand whole, unpaid, not reduced, not asked to be reduced, and were not paid, resolved, repaired or reduced in this batch. The final chapter's pencil is Nessa Pike's and she knows it and has said what to check.**

### Part

- **PART — The condition. (Ch 147 twice.)** The bare fact is carried twice in the second column with a reason against each line, **and neither carries a name.** **THE ONE NAMING VOLUME 03 IS ALLOWED WAS SPENT IN CHAPTER 137 AND NOTHING SPENT IT AGAIN.** The four lines above the named one are still nameless and are not struck out, the count of four in the first column in a ship's hand with no name against it stands, and a request to strike a line out of a column is refused.
- **PART — The Faultreader stage. (Ch 147, one seam, and the whole of it is the difference in what he did with it.)** He reads a break in the *Hearthwake's* garboard in six feet of water in the dark on the first call down: a plank cut and let go and mended over, mendings under mendings. **It identifies difference and it identifies nothing else, and this time he did not supply the rest, and the reason is on the page.** It is not a rank, nobody conferred it, nobody named it, it settles nothing, proves nothing, convicts nobody, and it put no crew behind a man. **No stage of any kind was taken in this batch and none may be.**
- **PART — Mara Venn. (Ch 145 and Ch 148.)** **Not on the crew, not asked for anything by this party, and present as a person who uses a room that is open to anybody on a Tuesday and a Friday morning.** She prices herself first, says the yard did not ask and would not have got a different figure, and does one thing at nothing **once** and says so in front of nine people so that it cannot be said for her. She gives six things and no seventh about the bells. **Her forty marks are unpaid, first after food, unpaid since the fifteenth of October, not reachable by the writ, and were not paid in this batch. She did not break with the Registry, which is Volume 4 and a different event, and no chapter of this batch fuses the two. She did not leave this harbor.**
- **PART — The third column. (Entries on the first and the fifteenth of August, and one day it was NOT filled.)** **It is not renamed, not scratched out and not quietly stopped.** About four hours' lateness on the first, **about an hour's on the fifteenth — the shortest since the fourth of April — and the party has entered that and is not going to make anything of it either way.** **It was not filled at all on Tuesday the eighth of August, the first time since the second of May that it has not been filled on a day, and the entry says why, and says the further thing: the party does not know who will fill that column next and has not appointed anybody and is not going to appoint anybody in a shed on a Wednesday night, and a column nobody fills is a column somebody else fills.**
- **PART — The harbor's reader. (Ch 146, one negative.)** **She did not say the reader thing a fourth time and the entry records that she did not and the reason is that the fourth time was to be in a different sentence and she had already said it three and there is no fourth form.** It is at four marks a week for a hand that will last a week, which is a wage and not a fee, and it is in no column in that book, and nobody in this harbor is hiring at that figure to read a slate back, **and the fourth wage week of the last five was not paid, and the first column says so with the figure against it.**
- **PART — The reeve. (Ch 146.)** **He spends authority a third time, at his own cost, and the price lands on the third lay again and on his own office's standing in a market. He says there is nothing in his book that will put a boat of his past the outer marks and nothing in the holder's either, and that this is the design. He is not a friend, not a hero, and the volume does not pretend he was right about everything. He has no second season in his keeping and a spent ledger, and he is not going to be on those steps again this month.** The plate was not mentioned, not asked about and not reached.
- **PART — Sella Brigg's warp. (Ch 141, Friday the twenty-eighth.)** A crew of Ashtide took about four hundred feet of her own warp off the bottom of this harbor for twenty-six marks and a day. **SHE HAS BEEN PAID BY HIM AND IS OWED EIGHT SHILLINGS AND TENPENCE BY THIS YARD, AND THE TWO FIGURES ARE NOT ONE FIGURE AND NEITHER HAS MOVED.**

### Carried

- **CARRIED — the coast road.** Not resolved, not explained, **not called a network by anybody in this batch, and not connected to the join and not connected to anything under this town.** Its figures are printed in a market and going to press in a city. **The man of fifty-four out of a storm belt is still coming in the autumn for nothing, and no instrument in this harbor can call him and none can stop him, and the yard did not go and look this batch and has entered that it did not. Five miles of that coast was not looked at and eleven miles of water was run to look at two stones and a bar.**
- **CARRIED — the glass storm.** Not used. Two written occurrences stand, in two hands on two pages that are not on top of each other, **and no cause is named for it in this batch or in any volume and nothing in either line knows anything.**
- **CARRIED — the delayed note.** **Eleven logged occasions and two empty answers and NEITHER MOVED IN NINE WEEKS AND NEITHER MOVED BY A MEASUREMENT. No occasion was entered in this batch. The delay is a hand's width of a second and has not moved and was not taken and was not written down, AND NOBODY IN THIS YARD HAS SAID WHY AND NOBODY IN THIS HARBOR HAS EVER SAID WHY AND THIS PARTY IS NOT GOING TO BE THE FIRST.** The answer inside the note is reserved to the Volume 15 midpoint.
- **CARRIED — the bell.** Fouled, a frame cut through the casting on one side, no tongue on the frame, two parts of a chain in a lead plug where the tongue was. **THE GOING OUT OF THE SECOND OF MAY IS DONE AND IS SPENT AND WAS NOT REPEATED, NO SECOND EIGHT MARKS WAS PAID, NO BOAT WENT OFF THE SOUTH END IN THIS BATCH, AND THE STANDING SENTENCE SAID IN A STREET ON THE THIRD OF MAY STANDS UNREVERSED AND IS RECITED IN CHAPTER 150 AS THE WHOLE OF THE FINAL CHAPTER'S LAST IMAGE.** Eight marks and a day of a boat owing since the twenty-third of May, unpaid, unargued, **not paid, not argued and not asked about.**
- **CARRIED — the lead plug. THE BELLWRIGHT WAS NOT ASKED AGAIN AND IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH AS A PERSON — NOT IN A SHED, NOT IN A BOAT, NOT IN A MARKET, NOT ON A MOLE AND NOT IN A ROOM. NO DIGIT IS PRINTED ANYWHERE IN IT. THE CHEST WAS NOT OPENED AND WAS NOT BROUGHT DOWN THE HILL AND IS NAMED ONCE, IN THE CHAPTER 150 READ-OUT, AS A THING THAT WAS NOT OPENED.** This is not a virtue and is not put forward as one: a yard that has undertaken not to ask does not ask, and the work did not need him.
- **CARRIED — custody, the compartment, the run under this town, the ninth-line mark, the marking on the shard, the three places with no joint, the coast road, the first echo's witness, Sera Mott, Alden Marr, and the plate at the fifth seam.** None was touched in this batch and none may be touched by it. **The eighth-clause plan was not reopened, no better letter was written, the plate was not referred to in any chapter of this batch, and the concealment is hidden: nobody asked the boy and nobody asked the conservator.**
- **CARRIED — labor, whole.** **Nothing in a bag; three marks owing to a man of Ashtide; thirty-one on the note and not re-written and its three counts unmoved; five marks of compressor's gland unpaid and useless because the thing after the gland is the hose; the tin empty since the twenty-eighth of April. Sixteen lines and sixty-four marks in the wage column with the fifth of March the oldest, eleven envelopes on a bench and not one of them anywhere in twelve weeks, and four wage weeks of the last five months not paid, the most recent being the fifteenth of August and being in the first column with the figure against it.** Forty marks of claim standing whole. Forty marks to a conservator of no office, first after food, nine months by her own figure. Eight shillings and tenpence to each of two women, in no sentence together. Eight marks and a day of a boat to a bellwright. Six pots of air this season at the yard's own two-mark rate, two of them in a boat past the outer marks. No delivery hose. A hand lead's line used three times and not past the outer marks. A *Sallow Reach* at the outer end of the second lay with a compressor of her own and forty fathoms of her own hose. One herring berth short. The ninth seam out with an end entered for the first of September. **A third lay shut four days in the first week of August for an examination with no money behind it.** The tide gang four men short. Fifteen cistern walks at no figure and the cistern holding since the nineteenth of June, walked by strangers on the thirty-first of July and the seventh of August and by this party on the third of August, and **three rota days deliberately not walked — the twenty-seventh of July, the tenth of August and the fourteenth — each with the reason on the page in the yard's own hand, and this party's own count of its own walks being thirteen since the end of May.** The fire party's hose dead. The light not on. No boat of this office's own since the twenty-eighth of April, and nothing in that office's book that will put one past the outer marks. The *Kittiwake* on the hard with no cradle. The hard at Ashtide. Nineteen winters in a file, in a letter and in a list, and a herring skipper said a sentence about it in a market and would not say it twice. The *Sea Wren* at the pile, **and this party has not thanked him and has not been asked to.** Neave Tarrant, nine winters, not found, one line, and this yard has not gone. Four of the eleven copies of a public sheet not reached and not going to be. A hand that will last a week at four marks a week, which is a wage and not a fee and is in no column in that book.

### Ruled

- **RULED — THE ONE NAMING OF THE CONDITION WAS SPENT IN CHAPTER 137 AND WAS NOT SPENT AGAIN. IT IS NOT AVAILABLE IN THIS VOLUME OR ANY OTHER.** The bare fact is on the page twice in this batch, with a reason, and without a name, and the four lines above the named one are still nameless and are not struck out.
- **RULED — THE BELLWRIGHT WAS NOT ASKED AGAIN, AND HE IS NOT IN ANY CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH AS A PERSON, AND NO DIGIT OF WHAT HE SAID ON THE SECOND OF JULY IS PRINTED IN ANY OF THEM, AND THE CHEST WAS NOT OPENED.** He was not asked because a yard that has undertaken not to ask does not ask, and because the work did not need him, and **this is recorded as a fact about the undertaking and not as a virtue.**
- **RULED — THERE ARE FOUR RETURNS AND THERE IS NO FIFTH, AND NO FIFTH WAS MADE IN THIS BATCH.** Dated lines stand for seventeen days of July and nine days of August, and for the eighth and ninth of August in the form that the party was past the outer marks on its own account, raised nothing, told no office and will tell no office. **A basket half out of a hatch in a boat is not a raising. The reporting clause is on the writ and the writ is bounded by the outer marks, so the clause does not reach that ground, and the party has entered that it told no office and knows what a reader of a page in five years will make of it.**
- **RULED — CAPTAIN HOLT'S POSTING IS NO LONGER OPEN. IT IS DECIDED ON THE PAGE WITH ITS DATES AND IT CAME BY THE WRIT**, which was ruled on the fifth of July, recited on the twenty-sixth and applied on the twelfth of August. It is not a command in this harbor, not an inspection of the harbor authority, not a commission, and puts nobody past the outer marks or the imperial line, and the yard checked that against its own entry and the check holds. **No later volume may invent a second channel or a different posting.**
- **RULED — THE CHANDLER'S NOTE IS NOT RE-WRITTEN AND IT IS SAID OUT LOUD, IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND, IN NINE PEOPLE'S HEARING, ON TUESDAY THE FIFTEENTH OF AUGUST, AND THAT IS ENTERED.** The three counts of weeks do not move and the refusal of the thirty-first of March stands unreversed.
- **RULED — *UNDER A NAVAL PAPER* IS SETTLED IN THE PROSE AND IS NOT AMBIGUOUS ON THE PAGE.** The *Vigil's* notice went up on the outside of whatever was there on the fourth of August; the yard's own sheet went up on the outside of whatever was there on the fifteenth; **therefore the yard's sheet is under the naval paper, in the sense that the naval paper is above it, and that is the arrangement and it has been the arrangement since the ninth of November, and the shipwright says so out loud and refuses to pretend a sheet under a naval paper is a sheet on its own.**
- **RULED — THE NINE PLACES ARE CARRIED AS THE PAGE CARRIES THEM AND ARE CALLED NOTHING.** Six by a builder's own figures at this harbor's own second seam with a day against each and no year; three as the books give them, being this harbor's own weir called the Sill, a village not on this coast and not on any chart anybody here has seen, and a sand and shell lay of this coast not on any chart in this harbor. **They are not a compact, the nine are not the Nine Harbors, and the word *Harbors* is not on the page anywhere.**
- **RULED — THE 28TH OF JULY IS A FRIDAY.** Two state files and the Batch 0005 prompt called it a Tuesday. **Chapters 138, 139 and 140 all establish that 26 July is a Wednesday, and Chapter 131 has the woman herself say that he will not do it on a Tuesday tide. THE CHAPTERS ARE RIGHT AND THE STATE FILES ARE WRONG, AND THE WARP IS TAKEN ON FRIDAY THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JULY IN CHAPTER 141.**

### Open by design

- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the bell-archive.** Five bells at about five and a half fathoms inside the four hundred yards. **No instrument in this harbor reaches that water, no money in this harbor reaches that water, and no trade on this coast whose work it is.** A later volume may supply a way, at a price, and the price will have to fall on somebody.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what the glass in the crevice, in the packing and in the tallow of a lead carries.** **Not read in this batch, and Mara Venn put the same wall up again in the market office on the fourth of August in a different form: she cannot tell anybody what is in that boat, and the trade that can is six marks and two days.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the two "everything in her" clauses on the *Vigil's* notices, and why a man writes a longer word in a longer notice.** A conservator said in a market office that she does not know and has entered that she does not know. **No person is identified and nothing is resolved.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the marked face on the mole's crown.** Unidentified, unexplained, not allowed to settle anything, **and not moved: neither object has moved in ninety days and one of the tarpaulins still has a corner off it since the seventeenth of June.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the three places with no joint.** Entered on the page of the fifteenth of August as the books give them, **and the party has entered that it does not know why the three are there and is not going to guess.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — who changed the rule.** Asked in a market on the thirteenth of August in about forty people's hearing and not answered, and the not-answering is on the page with the reason against it. **This is the volume's new-volume question and it is Volume 04's and Volume 05's business and not this volume's.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the figure on the bar of gravel, going to press in a city this month, wrong about one thing this yard cannot do one single thing about and has refused to make itself able to do.** Harl Dace is not in this batch and is not asked.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the first echo's witness.** Unnamed, unlocated, uninterviewed, not in this batch.

### What the Volume 03 close inherits, in plain terms

**The volume's five movements are all written. The climax is paid, the resolution is paid, the new-volume question is asked in a market and not answered, and the final chapter image is on the page. The volume's close is a SHORT thing and it must not open a Volume 04 thread by accident.** It is owed six items and nothing more.

1. **A WHOLE-VOLUME AUDIT** of Chapters 101–150 against `outline/volume-03.md` and its twenty-eight locks, and against `reviews/volume-02/volume-02-close.md`, which is the home of every ruling Volume 03 inherited. **THE REVIEWER MUST RE-DERIVE THE MONEY FROM THE CHAPTERS.** **IT MUST RULE ON LOCK L5, WHICH ENUMERATES THREE RETURNS AND SAYS THERE ARE THREE AND ONLY THREE, AGAINST FOUR RETURNS ON THE PAGE, THE FOURTH BEING THE FOURTEENTH OF JULY. THAT ITEM HAS BEEN FLAGGED SINCE BATCH 0004 AND IS STILL OPEN.**
2. **THE TWO AUTHORIAL QUESTIONS THAT WERE FLAGGED AND NOT APPLIED IN BATCH 0005** and are disposed of in `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0005 as written* § 8 items 7, 8, 9 and 10: why the *Vigil's* second notice says *everything in her*; whether any later volume supplies a way to five and a half fathoms and at whose expense; whether Holt's relief lands before or after the first of September; and **the attribution of the volume's closing sentence, which the outline calls a fisher's and which no fisher in Volume 02 is recorded as having said.**
3. **A WHOLE-VOLUME WORD COUNT, MEASURED.** Batch 0005 is **29,814** after its review repairs and 29,688 before them, and the per-chapter figures are in `state/current.md` and `state/chapter-summaries.md`.
4. **THE PLANNED ENDING IS UNTOUCHED AND MUST STAY UNTOUCHED.** No new antagonist, no new institution and no new final enemy was created in this volume, and the ninth-line mark, the Quietening, the Crown Seal, the Office of Returning Memory, the nine final routes, the compartment, the custody items, the lead plug's number, the run under this town, the coast road and the plate at the fifth seam are all exactly where Volume 02 left them.
5. **A HAND-OVER TO VOLUME 04 THAT NAMES THE SIX OPEN THREADS AND NO MORE:** Mara Venn's break with the Registry, which is Volume 4 and a different event from her leaving this crew; the third column and the boy and the pencil; the nine-line mark, which closes in Volume 5; the question about who changed the rule; the second season of the writ and the first of March; and the five bells at five and a half fathoms.
6. **`state/phase-ledger.json` IS A HUNDRED AND FIFTY CHAPTERS OUT OF DATE AND IS THE CONTROLLER'S FILE. IT WAS NOT EDITED, NO MARKER WAS WRITTEN, AND NO PROMPT, VOLUME FILE OR BATCH FILE WAS CREATED OR DELETED TO COMPENSATE FOR IT.** This remains the one open gate item and it is flagged, not fixed.

## Volume 03 close — the six open threads, and the hand-over to Volume 04, 27 September 2026

**VOLUME 03 IS CLOSED AND AUDITED. `reviews/volume-03/volume-03-close.md` is the audit and is the home of every ruling below and of every ruling Volume 03 deferred. `outline/volume-04.md` and `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001.md` are created, and `workspace/volume-04/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` is the one queued phase.**

**THE CLOSE OWES SIX ITEMS AND NOTHING MORE, AND A VOLUME 04 WRITER MAY NOT OPEN A SEVENTH BY ACCIDENT. Every other thing Volume 03 carried is written into `outline/volume-04.md` and its thirty locks and is not a seventh thread; it is standing material, and the reason for the split is that a hand-over that lists everything is a hand-over nobody reads.**

### The six, and nothing else

1. **MARA VENN'S BREAK WITH THE REGISTRY, WHICH IS VOLUME 4 AND IS A DIFFERENT EVENT FROM HER LEAVING THIS CREW. NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 03 FUSED THEM AND NO CHAPTER MAY FUSE THEM NOW.** She left this crew in her own words on Sunday the twenty-third of July, before anybody asked her. **She did not break with the Registry in Volume 03, she did not leave the harbor, and she is a person who uses a room that is open to anybody on a Tuesday and a Friday morning because that office is open to anybody and this party did not arrange it.** Her forty marks stand unpaid, first after food, since the fifteenth of October, and she put the figure of nine months on a page herself. She is not a holder, the reporting clause does not reach her, she said so in a room where it was entered, and **no chapter of Volume 04 may put her back on the crew as a way of resolving anything.**
2. **THE THIRD COLUMN, THE BOY, AND THE PENCIL.** He is off the stone since Monday the fifteenth of May 2026 and is not replaced, he is owed forty marks of claim standing whole and owed none of it, his hand has been going since the fourth of April, and **the yard's own book says in its own hand and with a date on the first of August that he cannot do the thing he was kept for, and that the party did not ask him and is not going to, and that a column in that book is not a wage — AND THAT ENTRY BINDS A LATER VOLUME AS MUCH AS THIS ONE.** The head of the third column keeps his hand and a request to scrape it out is refused in the same words it is refused for a line in any column. **The third column was not filled on the eighth of August, the first time since the second of May, and the party has entered that it does not know who will fill it next and has not appointed anybody and is not going to, and that a column nobody fills is a column somebody else fills. The last line of Chapter 150 is a pencil in this yard tonight and it is in somebody else's hand, and the reader who wants to know whose can have it from the seventh of August, in a shed, from the woman who told him not to write a sentence down.** The three nots of the eighth of June stand and nobody in that crew may ask him one word about the omissions he has entered, **and no Volume 04 chapter may hire a boy to read a position back at four marks a week and call that the same thing.**
3. **THE NINE-LINE MARK, WHICH CLOSES IN VOLUME 5 AND NOT BEFORE.** It was referred to twice in Volume 03 and refused twice, in the yard's own hand, and **the party has entered that if nine of anything is a row of nine then a man can count a row of nine and this party has counted one and is not going to count it twice, and Mara Venn has said the same thing a third time about a line on a bell and a line on a joint.** The nine fastenings in about seven feet of each of the six joints are not a mark of nine joined lines, and the nine places in six books are not the Nine Harbors, **and neither may be converted into either, and the three places with no joint are a different thing again and may not be fused with any of it.**
4. **THE QUESTION ABOUT WHO CHANGED THE RULE, WHICH WAS ASKED IN A MARKET IN THIS HARBOR ON SUNDAY THE THIRTEENTH OF AUGUST IN ABOUT FORTY PEOPLE'S HEARING AND WAS NOT ANSWERED, AND THE NOT-ANSWERING IS ON THE PAGE IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND WITH THE REASON AGAINST IT.** The questioner's words are *I am a boat and I have been on this water forty-one years* and *who changed the rule.* The answer is *I do not know*, and the reason is that **a man who has never heard the question says what rule and a man who has heard it says I do not know**, and that the yard is not going to go round the side of a limit four people put on their own account in the open air in front of forty witnesses, **and that the reason is this yard's decision of the fifth of July not to put a name on a thing it cannot check, which is the only thing this yard has had this year that nobody can take off it.** **NO OFFICE IN VEVR IS SHOWN THE DOCUMENT THAT WOULD ANSWER IT, THE NINE HARBORS ARE NOT NAMED AS A COMPACT, THE OFFICE OF RETURNING MEMORY IS NOT NAMED, AND THE QUIETENING AND THE CROWN SEAL ARE NOT NAMED. IT IS VOLUME 04'S OPENING AND VOLUME 05'S BUSINESS, AND IT IS NOT PERMISSION TO NAME THE COMPACT.**
5. **THE SECOND SEASON OF THE WRIT AND THE FIRST OF MARCH, AND THE FIRST OF SEPTEMBER, AND A WAGE THAT WAS NOT PAID AND THREE MARKS OWING TO A MAN OF ASHTIDE.** The writ runs to midnight on the first of March 2027 and its last line says the second season is not the harbor authority's to give and that the holder will be told when it has been decided and not before, and a paper about it comes up the hill and is read and answered on the day it comes, through the writ, which is the only channel and was ruled on the fifth of July and applied on the twelfth of August. **The ninth seam's mooring was out of service from the first of May with an end of it entered for the first of September, which is a Friday, and that date falls seventeen days after Volume 03 closed and is the first movement of Volume 04. A wage of four marks was not paid on Tuesday the fifteenth of August and the envelope was not made and the column stands at sixteen lines and sixty-four marks with the fifth of March the oldest, and three marks is owing to the master of the* Sallow Reach *for a day of work on the eighth, with a date on it, in the first column, in no column of the book.** The court cannot convict this party of a wreck and the Crown has to acknowledge a manifest discrepancy, and Calder Marr loses eligibility for the full Second Bell examination, **and it is not the writ that takes it and the writ is expressly not it.**
6. **THE FIVE BELLS AT ABOUT FIVE AND A HALF FATHOMS INSIDE THE FOUR HUNDRED YARDS, AND THE FOUR PEOPLE OF THE SILL WHO OWN THEM AND WHO HAVE NO BOAT, AND THE FACT THAT NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS HARBOR REACHES THAT WATER, NO MONEY IN THIS HARBOR REACHES THAT WATER, AND NO TRADE ON THIS COAST WHOSE WORK IT IS.** They went back into the water out of a man's hands on the eighth of August when he let go of a line round his own forearm, **and they are lost irreversibly and nobody else's finding is substituted for them and no Volume 04 chapter may raise them by argument, by right, or by a seal.** The four people are Nessa Vale, Awn Proude, Tom Kittow and Ewan Kittow, seventeen, **and they are alive, they have no boat, no wages and about nine marks between them, they have not asked this yard for anything, and the yard has entered that it would have liked to and had nothing.** And **the limit they wrote themselves and signed is a real limit and is not a formality: a bell-archive is not a thing that is read out, it is a thing that is struck, in a hall, in front of the people it is about, and there is nobody in this town it is about; it is not to be said in a shed, not into a book that goes out of this harbor, not to a man with a seal, not one word about who took their vessel off them, and not one name to any office in any city. SOMEBODY BEARS IT: the four bear it, and the holder bears it, because he asked for it to be lifted on the tenth of August, could not lift it, did not ask twice, and entered the asking on the page with a date.** A later volume may supply a way and the price must fall on somebody who is not the yard, **and the harbor authority may not supply one, no office in Veyr may supply one, and no person with a seal may say that anybody may go.**

### Carried forward in `outline/volume-04.md` and NOT a seventh thread

**The second-column bare fact and the spent naming of the twentieth of July, with the four nameless lines above it still four and still not struck out and the count of four in the first column with no name against it; the lead plug and the bellwright and the undertaking of the second of July that binds the yard and not the office and not the market and not the boy; the coast road with its four figures, the pilot's two figures and the Kettle that was not looked at; the delayed note's eleven logged occasions and two empty answers and a delay of a hand's width of a second that nobody has ever said why; the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers; the plate at the fifth seam and the refused plan of the seventeenth of January, whose mechanism is a change of politics in an office and not a better argument from a shed; the chart and its bell notation of the twenty-fifth of December, which went out of this harbor on the twenty-second of June into a vessel's keeping and is the only copy; the two objects of nobody's on the mole's crown, about nine feet apart, under two tarpaulins, one with a corner off it since the seventeenth of June, neither moved in ninety days, and the marking on one of them not identified and not going to be; the three places with no joint, owed a placement in a later volume as a fact and not as an identification; the shard of worked glass nobody can read, with six marks and two days on the page and the yard's inability to pay; the first echo's witness, who is in no chapter of Volume 02 and none of Volume 03 and none of Volume 04 either; Neave Tarrant, nine winters, out since the twenty-eighth of February, not found, one line; and the harbor's poverty, which is not fixed for anything and is not fixed in Volume 04 either.**

### The one lock ruling a Volume 04 writer must read before anything else

**LOCK L5, AS AMENDED BY THE VOLUME 03 AUDIT: THERE ARE FOUR RETURNS AND THERE IS NO FIFTH, AND LOCK L5'S OLD ENUMERATION OF THREE IS WITHDRAWN.** The full amended form is at `outline/volume-04.md` lock L5 and at `reviews/volume-03/volume-03-close.md` Part 2, Item 1. **The counting rule is unchanged: the day of a finding is not counted and the return falls due on the fourth day after it. All four were caught by the first limb of the clause by itself. Nothing is recalled, nothing is corrected, nothing is un-said and nothing is re-sent, and a document already in a circuit office's file cannot be withdrawn by a party deciding to amend its own bookkeeping.**

### Thread hygiene for the Volume 04 writer

**Six open threads. No seventh. The four flagged-not-applied items of Batch 0005 are disposed of in `state/continuity.md` § 1 item 14 and are not threads. The four structural findings that were recorded and not repaired — the nine chapters past the word guideline, the shape of Chapter 150's read-out, the slate wall's counter restarting at zero, and the wage column's May arithmetic — are facts about the chapters and are not threads either.** Phase status is the controller's alone and this file does not report it.

## Volume 04 planning repairs, 27 September 2026 — thread-level consequences, and the six threads are still six

**A reviewer pass over `outline/volume-04.md`, `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001.md` and the queued `workspace/volume-04/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` returned FIX with twenty-nine items. The full disposition is `state/continuity.md` § *Volume 04 planning-phase review repairs*. NO CHAPTER EXISTS, NO CARD WAS CUT, NO MOVEMENT BOUNDARY WAS MOVED AND NO PLANNED PLOT WAS ALTERED. These are the consequences for a Volume 04 writer and they are binding on the batch that is queued. THE SIX OPEN THREADS ARE THE SIX OPEN THREADS AND NO SEVENTH WAS OPENED BY ANY OF THIS.**

1. **THE THREE MARKS OWING TO PERRY OLLETT ARE ONE DEBT, HE IS NAMED ONCE IN THIS VOLUME, AND HE IS NOT PAID IN MOVEMENT 1.** The two forms of him in the planning files are reconciled in `outline/volume-04.md` lock L2: *a man of Ashtide* and *Perry Ollett of the* Sallow Reach *are one man*. **He may be named in Chapter 153 and in no other chapter of Volume 04, and no chapter may ask him a question, take a statement from him, send him a letter or send the yard up this hill to see him.** Chapter 153 used to pay three marks of a six-mark debt that never existed; **it now pays nothing, and the reason it pays nothing is in the first column with the figure and the date on it.** He is owed three marks and the volume is poorer for it and the debt is not going to be paid this month.
2. **THE THIRD LAY IS NOT USED AGAIN, AND THE COST OF THE NINTH ACKNOWLEDGEMENT HAS MOVED ONTO A MAN OF THE FIRST LAY.** The third lay lost four days of the best of its season in the first week of August to an examination of this mole's footing, has not been paid, is not going to be paid, and **is not going to be used again**. The ninth acknowledgement is entered at the yard's asking in Chapter 156, a day on the mole's toe is hired and paid for by a **woman of the second lay** at twelve of seventeen, **and the price falls on a man of the first lay who had the mole's toe booked for the same Monday and is out of a day and a day's work because this yard asked for the ninth.** The yard enters his name and the figure, does not pay it, and is not going to say sorry in a market. **The reeve is asked twice in his life and paid twice and says out loud, before anybody goes in the water, that he cannot pay a third time and is not going to be asked a third time.** This is the standing poverty condition of the volume being paid for and not softened.
3. **THE WAGE COLUMN HAS MOVED TWICE IN MOVEMENT 1 AND STANDS AT EIGHTEEN LINES AND SEVENTY-TWO MARKS, WITH THE FIFTH OF MARCH THE OLDEST.** A wage of four is paid on Tuesday the twenty-second of August out of a day of work, and a wage of four is paid on Tuesday the twenty-ninth out of a second day, **and the second of those is a figure in the yard's own book read out in Chapter 157 and not a scene, because no day of the batch is set on that day.** **THE BAG CLOSES THE BATCH AT A MARK AND TWELVE SHILLINGS AND FOURPENCE — the figure was wrong in this file until a reviewer pass measured it: a mark is a hundred and sixty pence and a shilling is twelve, so the shilling at the press on the twenty-fifth of August leaves a hundred and forty-eight pence and not nineteen of them, and every figure after that step was wrong in all four state files, in the card file and in `outline/volume-04.md`.** No wage was paid twice and no envelope was made for either, and **thirteen envelopes are on a bench and one week fallen due this year has no envelope standing against it.**
4. **THE MAN IN A COAT IS NOT NAMED IN THIS VOLUME, AND THAT IS A DECISION AND NOT A GAP.** Chapter 149 of Volume 03 has the holder tell him he does not know him and is not going to find out this month. **A Volume 04 chapter may not name him, may not have anybody in Morrow Quay learn his name, and may not have him take a coat off.** **Ambrose Sell is named wherever a clerk of the circuit office appears** — Chapters 152, 159 and 160 — so that the office has a face and the man in a coat is a channel.
5. **MOVEMENT 3's TURN AND MOVEMENT 5's DOCUMENT ARE BOTH STILL TO COME, AND THE PROMPT NOW SAYS SO.** **The phrase *risk management* is not on any page of this volume before Chapter 171**, no document in which the Registry accepts a removal is produced, quoted, summarised or described before then, and **the provisional public copy record is not obtained, not made, not released and not printed in Movement 1** — only its existence may be known, in Chapter 155, and what the yard publishes in its place is a request with a date on it and a refusal with no date on it. **The reading of the crew's book in a room is the volume's midpoint and is not in this batch**: Chapter 159 produces the book whole, puts a copy into a file in a city, and reads nothing out of it.
6. **THE DIVERGENCE FROM `outline/series.md` IS FLAGGED, NOT SILENT, AND THE ENDING IS UNTOUCHED.** The series outline plans the original anchor and an intimacy in this volume; `outline/volume-04.md` refuses both and says so, with a date, on its own face. **`outline/series.md` and `outline/ending.md` were not edited and no Volume 04 chapter may restore either beat.**
