- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers**, and the lead plug's number, and the run under this town, and the compartment, and the first echo's witness, and Neave Tarrant, nine winters, out since the twenty-eighth of February, not found, one line, and this yard has not gone.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — leaf 88.** A page in a file in a city that the copy of leaf 62 is to be read with, that nobody in this harbor has seen, that two people in this town can prove exists, and whose contents are not on a page of this book and are not going to be on one in this volume. **It is the mechanism by which the Registry uses work of a person of no office, and the yard has the number and the fact and nothing whatever about the thing, and the not-knowing is the whole of what the batch pays for it.**

### What Movement 4 inherits, in one place

1. **THE READING OF THE YARD'S OWN CREW'S BOOK IN A ROOM IS THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT AND IS CHAPTERS 181 AND LATER, AND IT HAS NEVER HAPPENED.** The book has been produced whole three times — twice in September and once on the sixteenth of October — and not one line of it has been read out of it in any room, and a copy of it has been in a file in a city since the ninth of September and cannot be recalled. **The party has entered, in advance, that it is coming, that it is not going to soften a line and not going to leave one out, and that the week of it is not in its keeping.** The second column has five lines that nobody in this harbor can check and the reasons against four of them are in the yard's own hand; the first column's bar of gravel entry is the yard's most checkable page and the yard's most exposed one.
2. **THE TURNS THAT ARE SPENT AND MAY NOT BE SPENT AGAIN: the page gone out of a file, the document in which an office accepts a removal, and the phrase *risk management*.** All three are on the page from the ninth, twelfth, fifteenth and sixteenth of October, and a later chapter may find the same two words in a second document and **may not present that as a new discovery.** **AND THE YARD CANNOT BE TOLD WHAT WAS ON LEAF 62 OR LEAF 88, MAY NOT PRODUCE EITHER, COPY EITHER OR ASK FOR EITHER BACK.**
3. **MARA VENN HAS LEFT THE REGISTRY AND HAS NOT JOINED THIS CREW, AND NEITHER IS A SACRIFICE TO THE OTHER AND NEITHER IS REPAIRED BY EITHER OF THEM GIVING THE OTHER ANYTHING.** She has no office, forty marks first after food unpaid since the fifteenth of October, six marks and two days unoffered, and a line in a city that says she will not be cited, quoted, produced or asked again. **SHE IS NOT COMING TO THE MARKET OFFICE ON A TUESDAY OR A FRIDAY MORNING. SHE IS NOT ON THE FOUR NAMES OF THE WRIT AND THE REPORTING CLAUSE DOES NOT REACH HER AND NEVER DID. THE YARD ASKED HER FOR ONE THING ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF SEPTEMBER AND WAS REFUSED, AND HAS ASKED HER FOR NOTHING SINCE AND IS NOT GOING TO.**
4. **HE STILL HAS NOT TOLD HER ABOUT THE SEVENTH OF AUGUST, AND THE NOT-TELLING IS FOUR ENTRIES ACROSS FOUR CHAPTERS IN THE SECOND COLUMN WITH A REASON AGAINST EACH LINE, AND ON THE SEVENTEENTH OF OCTOBER SHE ENTERED, THROUGH HIM, THAT SHE DID NOT ASK.** It is not paid, not repaired, and not going to be paid in this volume. **A LATER CHAPTER MAY NOT HAVE HIM TELL HER AS A CONFESSION, MAY NOT HAVE IT BE THE PRICE OF ANYTHING SHE GIVES HIM, AND MAY NOT HAVE HER ASK HIM FOR IT.**
5. **ELEVEN WORDS IN A RECORD IN A CITY SAYING THERE IS A WOMAN IN THIS HARBOR THE YARD WILL NOT NAME, entered by the holder in his own left hand and read back to him, and the entry of the eighteenth of September that this party does not put a name on a thing it cannot check is the authority for it and is not improved on.**
6. **THE YARD HAS FOUR THINGS AND NOT ONE OF THEM CAN BE PUT IN FRONT OF ANYBODY:** a number off a copying job sheet, a counterfoil and a fair copy in two other people's keeping, a file on a second floor it has not asked for, and a woman it cannot pay. **THE SHIPWRIGHT SAID THAT OUT LOUD IN A DOORWAY ON THE TWELFTH AND IT IS ON THE PAGE AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE IMPROVED ON IN A MARKET.**
7. **THE THIRD COLUMN HAS NOT BEEN FILLED SINCE THE FIRST OF AUGUST, THE SHIPWRIGHT HAS NINE SENTENCES IN HER HEAD AND HAS SAID WHY SHE IS NOT WRITING THEM, THE HEAD OF THAT COLUMN KEEPS THE BOY'S HAND, AND A REQUEST TO SCRAPE IT OUT IS REFUSED. NOBODY MAY BE APPOINTED TO IT.**
8. **THE ONE NAMING OF THE CONDITION WAS SPENT IN CHAPTER 164 AND IS NOT AVAILABLE AGAIN. THE FOUR NAMELESS LINES ABOVE THE NAMED LINE OF THE TWENTIETH OF JULY ARE STILL FOUR AND ARE NOT STRUCK OUT.**
9. **THE COAST ROAD, THE PILOT, AND THE KETTLE.** Both figures for the bar of gravel are in type in a city side by side, neither is withdrawn, **this volume does not decide which of them is right and Movement 4 may not decide it either.** The refused license is carried as a cost and is not re-decided. The Kettle was not looked at.
10. **THE FOUR RETURNS ARE FOUR AND NOT FIVE, AND THE DAY OF A FINDING IS NOT COUNTED AND THE RETURN IS DUE ON THE FOURTH DAY AFTER IT. A RETURN STATES A FACT AND NOT A CONCLUSION, AND ALL FOUR RETURNS WERE CAUGHT BY THE FIRST LIMB BY ITSELF, AND THE PARTY'S POSITION THAT THE FIRST LIMB IS ENOUGH IS ON THE PAGE AND IS NOT IMPROVED ON. THE INQUIRY'S POWER TO SEAT ONE OF TWO DISPUTANTS IS NOT THE WRIT'S POWER AND THE TWO INSTRUMENTS MAY NOT BE CONFUSED BY ANY PERSON.**
11. **THE HARBOR'S POVERTY, AND IT IS WORSE.** A mark and twelve shillings in a bag at the close against a mark and twelve shillings and fourpence at the open, a wage of four not paid on the sixth of October, a day of about three marks refused by the yard itself, a day that could not be booked for want of men, three weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them, one herring berth out, the north berth out, a light not on, a tide gang four short, a hard at Ashtide unanswered, a fire party's hose dead, nineteen winters in a list in a city, the *Sea Wren* at the pile with this party not having thanked him, **and the cistern behind the engine house charged nineteen times since the third of August and walked on none of the nineteen, the run being fourteen on the first of October, fifteen on the fifth, sixteen on the eighth, seventeen on the twelfth, eighteen on the fifteenth and nineteen on the nineteenth, and the fifteenth being the one charge of the month this party knows of at second hand, from a man of the third lay at the head of the dock on the seventeenth.**
12. **A REPO AND A COLD BEAT ARE OWED AND ARE NOT OWED YET.** `outline/volume-04.md` § *Costs this volume may not waive* names *at least one injury to a working crew member with a real cost and a real bill, and one bad day, and the tide-sickness continuing in the second column with a reason against the line and no name in it.* **NEITHER IS RECORDED AS PAID IN MOVEMENTS 1, 2 OR 3 AND THE VOLUME STILL OWES IT, AND IT IS STANDING MATERIAL FOR MOVEMENT 4 OR 5 AND IS NOT A CARD.**
13. **And the six open threads, unchanged in number.** Mara Venn's break with the Registry, which is paid and which does not close the thread of what is in a file in a city; the third column, the boy and the pencil; the nine-line mark, which closes in Volume 5; the question about who changed the rule; the second season of the writ and the first of March and the unpaid wages and the three marks; and the five bells at five and a half fathoms with the four people of the Sill who have no boat.

### Thread hygiene for the Volume 04 writer

**Six open threads. No seventh. The volume's central pressure is on the page, Movement 2's turn is landed, Movement 3's turn is landed and spent, and Movement 4's reversal is landed and paid. The new items listed under *Opened or advanced by this batch* are the volume's own planned work in `outline/volume-04.md` § *Movement 4* and are not a seventh hand-over. Phase status is the controller's alone and this file does not report it. The one queued phase is `workspace/volume-04/batch-0005/PROMPT.md` and nothing beyond it exists.


## Volume 04, Batch 0004 (Chapters 181–190) — as written, Movement 4, the faultreader in a room, which is the volume's midpoint

**37,052 words across ten chapters, Thursday the twenty-second to Tuesday the tenth of November 2026, measured with `wc -w` including headings, against the 2,200–3,200 guideline. MOVEMENT 4 IS COMPLETE, THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT IS PAID, AND THE REVERSAL HAS LANDED. THE BATCH IS REPAIRED AT ITS REVIEW AND THE REPAIRS ARE `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0004 review repairs*; the figure of 36,882 that stood here is withdrawn and is 37,052 after sixteen repairs, none of which moved a scene, a beat or a cost. The full accounting is `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0004 as written (Chapters 181–190), Volume 04*, the duplication audit is § 10 of that section, and the shape of the batch and the four chapters that are past the guideline are § 11 and the head of `state/chapter-summaries.md`. The six open threads are the six open threads and this batch opened no seventh. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED, NO SCENE WAS CUT, NO COST WAS DROPPED AND NO PLANNED PLOT WAS ALTERED.**

### Carried and moved

- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE REVERSAL. THE CREW'S BOOK OF THIS YARD WAS READ OUT IN A ROOM IN MORROW QUAY, WHOLE AND UNSHORTENED, FROM THE FIRST OF MAY TO THE LAST PAGE, IN THE ORDER IT IS IN, IN THE HOLDER'S OWN VOICE, FOR THREE HOURS AND FIFTY MINUTES, ON WEDNESDAY THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF OCTOBER 2026, AND THE SITTING RAN TWO DAYS.** The clerk asked twice for a thing to be read a second time and both times it was read a second time. The two columns were read out. The third column at the head of a page, fourteen lines in the right hand of a boy of twenty-two dated from the second of May, was NOT read by the holder; the holder gave it up in a sentence in the room and did not take it back, and the clerk read it in a flat voice and said in the room that this office cannot make that boy come and cannot make anybody ask him. **NO COPY OF ANY RECORD WAS MADE IN THAT ROOM AND NONE WAS GIVEN TO ANY PARTY AND NONE WAS ASKED FOR. THE READING IS PAID AND IS NOT AN EVENT TWICE.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE SEAM, ON THE NOTICE OF THE TENTH OF SEPTEMBER, IN A ROOM, ON THE TWENTY-NINTH OF OCTOBER, AND IT SAYS ONE THING.** That a sheet of paper has been in two hands at two times. **It does not say which hand is right, or why, or whose the two hands were, and the man who put it there said so in the same breath and would not say more. FOUR PHYSICAL GROUNDS ARE ON THE PAGE AND NONE OF THEM NAMES A PERSON.** **AND A CLERK OF THE CIRCUIT OFFICE SAID IN THE SAME ROOM THAT HE HAD HIMSELF HAD THAT PAPER SINCE THE TENTH OF SEPTEMBER, COULD SEE THE TWO INKS, AND HAD NOT NEEDED THE HOLDER OF THE WRIT OF THE FIRST OF MAY TO COME UP A HILL AND POINT AT THEM, AND ENTERED THE FACT IN THAT ORDER, THE CLERK FIRST AND THE HOLDER SECOND, AND GAVE HIS REASON, WHICH IS THAT IF THE FACT IS LEFT OUT OF THE RECORD THEN IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS THE ONLY THING IN THE BUNDLE ANYBODY IS PROUD OF IS A THING A MAN OF THIS HARBOR PUT THERE.** He was asked what it means and answered *I do not know* three times and would not say it a fourth time in a different way, and the clerk entered it. **A SEAM IDENTIFIES DIFFERENCE AND NOT TRUTH, IT WAS USED THE THIRD TIME IN THIS VOLUME, AND IT MADE HIM LESS CREDIBLE AND NOT MORE, WHICH IS WHAT THE VOLUME OUTLINE SAID IT WOULD DO. NEITHER THE SEAM NOR ITS GROUNDS MAY BE REPEATED AS AN EVENT AND NO LATER CHAPTER MAY ADD A GROUND THAT IDENTIFIES A PERSON.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE ANSWER OF THE EIGHTEENTH OF JULY, READ BACK IN A ROOM IN THE HOLDER'S OWN WORDS BY A CLERK WHO WAS NOT DOING A PART, TOGETHER WITH THE LINE THAT SAYS THE PARTY DID NOT STOP HIM AND THAT IT WAS WRONG IN THREE PARTS OUT OF FOUR AND HAS NOT TAKEN IT BACK.** The clerk entered that a page in a book and a sentence in a street are not the same instrument and that they are both going to be in the same bundle in about four years and that he is not the man who decides that they go in separate rooms. **The holder said in the room that he said five things in that market and that four of them are in his book and that the fifth he cannot produce, and said so in the room instead of not saying it. HE DID NOT DENY IT.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE SECOND COLUMN'S REASONS DO NOT GO INTO A RECORD, AND THAT IS THE MOVEMENT'S MECHANISM.** A clerk said in the room, in his own trade's words and not as a favour, that a record has a column and the column is the width of his page, and that if he takes four words out of a man's paragraph then in about four years a man in a city thinks the man wrote four words, and he did not. **The party had worked the same thing out in its own shed on Sunday the twenty-fifth of October, three days before the sitting, and entered it in the second column with a reason. FIVE CHECKLESS LINES WENT INTO A RECORD IN A CITY WITHOUT THE REASON AGAINST ANY OF THEM, FOUR OF THEM WITH NO NAME AT ALL, AND ONE OF THEM A LINE OF THE FOURTEENTH OF AUGUST OF WHICH THE MAN WHO WROTE IT CAN BRING UP THE FIRST FOUR WORDS. THIS IS THE VOLUME'S METHOD SPENT IN A HARDER CURRENCY AND MOVEMENT 5 MAY NOT REPEAT IT AS A DISCOVERY.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE REVERSAL'S OWN SENTENCE, SAID OUT LOUD IN A SHED ON THE THIRTIETH OF OCTOBER: that the second column is now the most dangerous column in this harbor, and that the limit four people of this coast wrote themselves and signed is not in the record because a limit is a reason and reasons do not go into a record, and cannot be put there, because this yard holds a writ and a writ is a channel and a man with a writ is the thing that limit was written against.** The four of the Sill are alive with no boat and no wages and about nine marks between them, their limit stands, they are named nowhere as persons in this batch and were asked nothing, and the holder did not ask twice to have it lifted and did not ask at all this month.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE SPINE. HE STILL HAS NOT TOLD HER, AND THE NOT-TELLING IS FIVE ENTRIES AND A FIFTH OCCASION NOT TO.** The decision on the third of October, the not-telling and the reason on the fourth, her line of the seventeenth of October that she was not going to ask him, a fourth occasion in a room on the twenty-eighth and twenty-ninth, and a fifth entry on the tenth of November with the reason of the fourth given again and the undertaking not to invent a second one. **IT IS NOT A CONFESSION, NOT A RELIEF, NOT A PRICE, NOT A TRADE, AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE ONE IN THIS VOLUME. SHE ASKED HIM FOR NOTHING AND HE ASKED HER FOR NOTHING.**
- **CARRIED, AND ADVANCED — the third column, the boy and the pencil.** **The third column was not filled on any day of this batch and nobody was appointed to it. NOBODY ASKED IN THE ROOM WHO FILLS IT — the claim that the clerk asked and that the answer was nobody is withdrawn and was wrong: he read the fourteen lines in a flat voice, said that the office cannot make the boy come and cannot make anybody ask him, entered that, and asked nothing else about it. The *who fills it* argument is in the shed on Wednesday the fourth of November, between the two of them, and she said no twice and gave the reason and would not give it again.** The head of the column keeps the boy's hand. The three nots stand and are entered in the first column of Chapter 187 in the same breath. Forty marks stand whole. The party entered on the fourth of November that it does not know where the boy is and has not asked and is not going to send for him. **A column nobody fills is a column somebody else fills, and the somebody else is a clerk with a pen, and that is on the page and was not improved on.**
- **CARRIED, AND UNTOUCHED — the nine-line mark.** Not referred to, not named, not explained, and not asked about in this batch. **It closes in Volume 5 and not before, and the three places with no joint are a different thing and were not converted into either.**
- **CARRIED, AND UNTOUCHED — the question about who changed the rule.** Asked in a market in this harbor on Sunday the thirteenth of August and not answered. **NOTHING IN THIS BATCH ANSWERS IT AND NOTHING IN THIS BATCH ASKS IT AGAIN, AND NO CHAPTER REMARKS ON THE QUESTION OF WHO DRAFTED THE FOURTH PARAGRAPH OF THE NOTICE OF THE TENTH OF SEPTEMBER — THE SEAM WAS PUT ON THAT PAPER AND IT SAID NOTHING WHOSE HANDS.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID TWICE — the second season of the writ, the first of March, the unpaid wages and the three marks.** The second season is a named absence and the paper served in a doorway on the twenty-third of October expressly does not touch it, grant it, refuse it or deal with it. **On Tuesday the third of November the wage of four marks did not go in and the envelope was not made — the bag held eight shillings and sixpence against a wage of six hundred and forty, so it was **five hundred and thirty-eight pence** short, and the figure of six hundred and thirty-eight that two chapters and a state file carried for this batch was arithmetically wrong and is corrected** — and the number of weeks fallen due this year with no envelope standing against them is FOUR — the fifteenth of August, the fifth of September, the sixth of October and the third of November — entered in the same breath in every place the figure is given. THREE MARKS ARE STILL OWING TO A MAN OF ASHTIDE FOR THE EIGHTH OF AUGUST AND NOT A SHILLING HAS BEEN PAID, he was not named in this batch, and no chapter of it asked him a question.**
- **CARRIED, AND UNTOUCHED — the five bells at about five and a half fathoms inside the four hundred yards.** Not raised, not offered, not argued, not promised, and not mentioned as anything anybody can do.

### Opened or advanced by this batch

- **NEW, AND PAID — THE VOLUME'S UNPAID INJURY AND BAD DAY ARE PAID, AND THEY ARE PAID, AND THEY ARE NOT TO BE PAID AGAIN.** **A copper union on a wharf's discharge let go under pressure on Friday the twenty-third of October at about half past two in the afternoon and the pump's handle came round through the quarter-turn that is in it and struck the left forearm of this yard's own shipwright across the bone about two inches above the wrist, and she said before anybody had said anything that it was the bone and not the joint.** Her figure is three shillings and sixpence, charged and paid on the twenty-fourth, and a quarter of a year out of the left side of a shipwright, and both of those are on ONE LINE in the first column in her own hand and in his, with the reason that a bone is not a bill and a bill is not a bone and a man who has the two in one line will end up arguing about the wrong one. Six weeks of strapping, the strapping off on the twenty-first of November and not before. **NOT PAID BY A DIVE, AND NO PERSON OF THIS YARD WAS IN ANY WATER ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH.** A day of work at the second seam's own face on the sixth of November went slower than a day with three on it because the jobs were swapped and the holder ran her pump, and a wharf day of about three marks offered on Sunday the eighth was refused by this yard. **MOVEMENT 5 MAY NOT USE ANOTHER INJURY AS ITS OWN COST.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE COST OF A HEARING AS A BODY'S TIME, ENTERED IN THE SECOND COLUMN WITH A REASON AND NO FIGURE, BECAUSE THERE IS NOT ONE IN THIS HARBOR.** Two days in a room on the twenty-eighth and twenty-ninth, neither of them on a mole, neither of them earning, and the whole of what came out of them going on a page in a city. A man of the **second** lay stood on the mole's steps on the twenty-sixth of October because that office put him there for the two days of the sitting and is out of a Tuesday on the water, and that loss is in no ledger because that office's ledger is spent, and **the yard asked him a question and made no request of any office and entered why.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A FIGURE ON A MARKET WALL MADE WORTH LESS BY A ROOM, SAID OUT LOUD BY A MAN WHO WAS NOT SAYING THE YARD WAS WRONG.** **Gwil Lasker, thirty-six, of the first lay, a boat of about a hundred and twenty tons, said at the head of this dock on Sunday the first of November that a yard which has been in a room and come out with a clerk saying that a clerk could see what the man could see is a yard whose figures are worth what the clerk's are, and gave his name before he was asked for it.** The figure of two and three-quarters on the flood and two and a quarter on the ebb was not withdrawn and is not going to be, the pilot's two and a half was not withdrawn, and neither can check the other and this volume does not decide which is right.
- **NEW, AND PAID — A NAME REFUSED AT A WALL TO A MAN WITH A LETTER FROM A CITY IN HIS COAT.** On Thursday the fifth of November, before the market was off the boards, a man of the first lay read the reason on the page of a conservator's unsigned finding out loud and asked this yard for the name, and the yard said no in about four minutes and gave the whole of it. **HE SAID THAT IN JULY THE SAME ANSWER HAD SOUNDED LIKE A YARD BEING CAREFUL AND THAT ON THE FIFTH OF NOVEMBER IT SOUNDS LIKE A WALL, AND THAT HE WOULD RATHER HAVE THE PAGE IN A CITY THAN THE WALL, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO PRETEND HE WOULD RATHER HAVE A YARD THAN A WALL BECAUSE HE HAS BEEN IN FOUR ROOMS IN THIS HARBOR THIS MONTH. THE HOLDER AGREED WITH HIM AND ENTERED AGAINST THIS PARTY THAT HE HAS NOT GOT A REASON FOR THAT PREFERENCE WHICH IS WORTH ANYTHING. THE FINDING WAS NOT SIGNED, WAS NOT TAKEN DOWN AND WAS NOT IMPROVED ON, AND THE MAN IS UNNAMED AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR A NAME.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE FIGURE AT THE HEAD OF THE CHAPTERS' MONEY, AND IT IS A BONESETTER.** A mark and twelve shillings in the bag on the twentieth of October and a mark and eight shillings and sixpence on the tenth of November, which is three hundred and four pence and two hundred and sixty-two pence, and the whole of the difference is three shillings and sixpence and it is a bonesetter's bill on the twenty-fourth. **THE WHOLE OF WHAT THIS YARD HAS SPENT ON ITSELF IN TWENTY-ONE DAYS IS A BONE, AGAINST FOUR PENCE OF POSTAGE IN THE TWENTY DAYS BEFORE THAT. NO FIGURE IN THE BAG EXCEEDED SIX MARKS AND TWELVE SHILLINGS AT ANY POINT IN THIS BATCH.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — HESSA BARLOW, FORTY-FOUR, A BONESETTER OF THIS HARBOR, WITH A ROOM AT THE BACK OF A CHANDLER'S SHOP OFF THE MARKET.** Named, ordinary, in her own trade's words, not a villain, not on any ladder, would not be thanked, came back on the fourteenth day and did not charge for it, and said the whole of the rest of the cost in her own figure and would not put it in her book. **She is the named working person the volume's unpaid injury fell on, and she has a real cost and a real bill against her in the first column in two hands.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — FENN ARDLE, FORTY-THREE, OF THE SECOND LAY, WITH A BOAT OF ABOUT SEVENTY TONS AND A BOY OF SEVENTEEN ON THE SECOND HAND.** The price of the reeve's spent ledger in this batch, and it falls on a man of the second lay and not on the third lay's four days and not on a man of the first lay's Monday a second time. He said in about eleven people's hearing that a thing nobody has written down is a thing that did not happen in about four years.
- **NEW, AND PAID — GWIL LASKER.** Named, ordinary, in his own trade's words, not a villain, not on any ladder. He has crossed this bar twenty-one springs and has never once said out loud that the yard's figure was wrong, and at twenty-two he took a sounding off that bar off a master's own figure at that master's own asking, was a foot and a half out in a slack water, did not tell him, and the said master had it himself inside a month off a man out of a city, and neither of them has said one word about it to the other since. **THE ERROR IS HIS OWN. It was not until the Batch 0004 review that this file gave him the holder's error — the word *chain*, and wrong in three parts out of four of the same sentence — which is Calder Marr's, and two men carrying the same mistake is the one thing this volume's reversal turns on.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — ORS KEST, ONCE, IN THE OPEN AIR, ON THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF OCTOBER.** He said he had put a man on those steps and told him on the Friday and not on the Monday morning because a man told on a Friday has a Saturday to be angry about; that the loss is not in his ledger because that ledger is spent; that a thing which cannot be entered cannot be produced and a thing which cannot be produced cannot be argued about; and that the repair account of that office has been empty since the middle of February and he would rather say so to about eleven people than have it found out in a market in March. **THE YARD TOOK NOTHING, WAS GIVEN NOTHING AND ASKED FOR NOTHING.**
- **NEW, AND PAID, AND IT IS A BEGINNING AND NOT A STAGE — THE BOOK OF HOURS.** Sixteen lines over three nights of the thirtieth and thirty-first of October and the first of November: nine pumps, four boats out, three boats in, the hour against each and NO REASON AGAINST ANY OF THEM. **The ship's hand said on the page that this is the first thing anybody learns about a listening buoy and that the rest of it needs a crew that keeps the hours, and that there is no crew, and that she is not going to let him have the other thing. THE STAGE WAS NOT TAKEN AND ITS COSTS OF MAINTENANCE, PUBLIC EXPOSURE AND DEPENDENCE ON A TRAINED CREW ARE NOT DISCHARGED.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE VOLUME'S FORWARD PULL, ENTERED IN THE FIRST COLUMN ON THE TENTH OF NOVEMBER.** **THE QUESTION STANDING ON THE TABLE IS A DOCUMENT THIS PARTY HAS NEVER SEEN AND HAS NEVER ASKED FOR: a copy of a record about an object, with the limits the record itself carries printed on the face of the copy, which would show that a manifest was altered and would not show who altered it, and which the copy itself would say so on its own face, and which a man of the first lay of this harbor cannot check.** The party entered that it is not applying for one this month and why, and that it is not saying no to it in four years and is not saying yes either, and that the release of that copy is not in this yard's keeping and has never been in it. **NO COPY WAS OBTAINED, MADE, RELEASED, PRINTED, QUOTED, SUMMARIZED OR DESCRIBED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS. ITS RELEASE IS THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX AND IS MOVEMENT 5.**

### Ruled

- **RULED — NO POWER STAGE OF ANY KIND WAS TAKEN AND NONE MAY BE.** The Faultreader stage is spent, is not re-taken, is not improved, and **the word is on no page of these ten chapters.** One seam was put, on one document, at the midpoint, and it said one thing. **HE WENT IN NO WATER IN THIS BATCH AND NO PERSON OF THIS YARD WAS IN ANY WATER ON ANY DAY OF IT.**
- **RULED — THE CONDITION IS NAMED NOWHERE IN THIS BATCH.** It is carried once, in the second column of Chapter 189, as a bare fact with a reason against it and without a name: a man stood in a room for two days and read out of his own book for three hours and fifty minutes, and on the Wednesday night he did not sleep and on the Thursday night he did, and the party entered both halves of that and is not going to name what it is in a shed. **The four lines above the named line of the twentieth of July are still four, are still nameless, and are not struck out, and the one naming Volume 04 was allowed was spent in Chapter 164 and is not available again.**
- **RULED — THE BELL IS NOT AN INSTRUMENT AND WAS NOT TOUCHED.** No boat went off the south end, no second eight marks was entered as paid, the phrase *a tongue half worn through* does not occur, the bellwright was not asked, he is in no chapter of this batch as a person, no digit of anything he said is printed, and the chest was not opened and was not brought down the hill.
- **RULED — THE LEAD PLUG IS UNTOUCHED.** Not asked about, not printed, not opened, and not brought down the hill.
- **RULED — THE CUSTODY ITEMS ARE UNTOUCHED.** Three items, three numbers, no digit quoted, none returns, none is asked for, the municipal claim on the third stands, and **the word does not occur once in the ten chapters of this batch.** The live question is not answered and is not put to anybody.
- **RULED — NO RETURN WAS MADE AND THERE IS NO FIFTH.** A finding of worked glass was made on no day of this batch and nothing was raised on any day of it, and the reporting clause was not touched and no day was reported to any office.
- **RULED — NO PERSON, BOAT OR LEAD OF THIS YARD WENT INSIDE THE FIRST CLAUSE OF THE NOTICE OF THE THIRTY-FIRST ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH.** The plate at the fifth seam was not reached, not measured, not touched and not asked about, the eight-clause plan of the seventeenth of January was not reopened, and **the plate note is OPEN and is not required to close in this batch.**
- **RULED — THE MAN IN A COAT IS NOT NAMED AND MAY NOT BE NAMED IN THIS VOLUME. HE IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH AND IN NO ROOM IN IT.** The commissioned officer of the Crown Navy who applied by letter for leaf 62 on the first of September is also not named, not described, and not guessed at.
- **RULED — AMBROSE SELL IS NAMED WHEREVER A CLERK OF THE CIRCUIT OFFICE APPEARS**, in Chapters 181 and 184, and **he said neither of the two things he said in September and in October a third time, he did not name the magistrate, and the name of the man in a coat is on no page of this batch and no person in this batch learned it.**
- **RULED — THE SLATE WALL'S COUNTER DID NOT RESTART AND IS TWENTY-FIVE, TWENTY-SIX AND TWENTY-SEVEN** on the twenty-sixth of October, the second of November and the ninth, all bare, all with the clerk keeping his own page, and no sitting held on any of them. The last figure of October was twenty-four and is Batch 0003's.
- **RULED — THE GRID. CHAPTERS 171 TO 190 OF THIS VOLUME RUN ON A GRID THREE DAYS OUT FROM THE ONE `outline/volume-04.md` PRINTED IN LOCK L1 BEFORE THE BATCH 0004 REVIEW, THE CHANGE ENTERS WITH CHAPTER 171, AND LOCK L1 HAS BEEN AMENDED AT ITS FOOT.** **A WRITER OF MOVEMENT 5 TAKES ITS DAYS FROM CHAPTER 190 AND FROM THE CHAPTERS AFTER IT AND NOT FROM THE TABLES IN LOCK L1: Monday the ninth of November 2026 is a Monday, the Mondays are the sixteenth, the twenty-third and the thirtieth of November, the wage Tuesdays are the seventeenth and the twenty-fourth of November and the first of December, the rota is a Monday and a Thursday so the nineteenth and twenty-sixth of November and the third of December are rota days, and the hearing room's slate wall is wiped on every Monday with the chapters' counter, which does not restart and has the ninth of November at twenty-seven.** **NO CHAPTER FROM 191 ONWARD MAY RESTATE A WEEKDAY FOR ANY DAY BEFORE THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER, MAY STATE THE YEAR, OR MAY MOVE A SINGLE DAY OF CHAPTERS 151 TO 190.** The amendment carries the full statement of what the chapters carry, the place the change falls, and the three reasons it was not repaired in the text, and `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0004 review repairs* item 1 is the authority.
- **RULED — THE MARKET WALL AND THE HEARING ROOM ARE NEVER THE SAME DAY, VERIFIED A FIFTH TIME.** The hearing room is used on two days, the twenty-eighth and the twenty-ninth of October; the market wall is touched once, on the fifth of November, in the open air; the market office is used on no day of this batch.
- **RULED — THE READ-OUT DEVICE WAS USED ONCE IN THIS BATCH, at the head of this dock on the evening of the tenth of November, and the reading of the crew's book in Chapter 184 IS NOT A USE OF IT and the state files record the distinction. THE READ-BACK IS NOT THE READ-OUT AND RAN IN FOUR OF THE TEN CHAPTERS, 181, 185, 188 AND 190, and not in any of the other six; **the figure of one that stood here and in three other binding files is withdrawn and was wrong**, and a plain string search for *read it back*, *read them back* and *read back after her* returns `chapter-0181.md:77`, `chapter-0185.md:49`, `chapter-0188.md:63` and `chapter-0190.md:55`. The clerk reading an entry back in a doorway or a room in Chapters 181 and 184 is his own trade's practice and is not this device.**
- **RULED — ALDEN MARR IS AT THE FAR BENCH ON SIX DAYS OF THIS BATCH, BEING THE TWENTY-SECOND AND THE TWENTY-SIXTH OF OCTOBER, THE THIRTIETH OF OCTOBER, AND THE SECOND, THE SIXTH AND THE NINTH OF NOVEMBER, AND ON NO OTHER DAY OF IT,** not asked for anything on any of the six and asking nothing on any of the six, and Calder Marr did not go over to him on any of the six. **THE FIGURE OF SIX IS COUNTED FROM THE PROSE AND WRITTEN FROM THE COUNT, WHICH IS THE RULE THE BATCH 0002 REVIEW LEFT AFTER TWO FAR-BENCH FIGURES HAD TO BE CORRECTED AGAINST THE CHAPTERS. The tin cup of the eighteenth of October is not repeated and is not improved on.**
- **RULED — THE PHRASE *RISK MANAGEMENT* IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH, AND THE PAGE THAT WENT OUT OF A FILE IS NOT REPEATED AS A DISCOVERY OR AS A SCENE.** Chapter 188 re-enters, as a carried fact and not as a new thing, that the yard holds two documents and not one and that a form is a form whichever office writes it, and gives the same reason as the entry of the twelfth of October and does not improve on it.
- **RULED — THE PROVISIONAL PUBLIC COPY RECORD WAS NOT OBTAINED, MADE, RELEASED, PRINTED, QUOTED, SUMMARIZED OR DESCRIBED.** It is named once, in the forward pull of Chapter 190, as a document the party has never seen and has never asked for, together with what it would show and what it would not show, and that is the whole of it.

### Open by design

- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the answer inside the delayed note.** Eleven logged occasions and two of them with no call in them, **neither advanced in this batch by arithmetic or by a measurement, no occasion entered, no measurement taken**, the delay a hand's width of a second and unmoved, and nobody in this yard and nobody in this harbor has said why. **The answer is reserved to the Volume 15 midpoint.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the release of the provisional public copy record.** It exists; the yard may apply in writing at its own cost for a copy of any record; **it has not applied, and the first column of Chapters 178 and 190 carry the reason, and Chapter 190 adds that the party is not saying no to it in four years and is not saying yes either. The copy is not made, not released, not printed, not quoted and no date attaches to any of it. ITS RELEASE IS THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX AND IS MOVEMENT 5.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — who drafted the fourth paragraph of the notice of the tenth of September.** Somebody has read a market wall of this harbor. The yard does not know who and is not going to guess. **A SEAM WAS PUT ON THAT PAPER IN A ROOM IN THIS BATCH AND IT SAID NOTHING WHOSE HANDS, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF WHAT THE BATCH DID WITH THE QUESTION, AND IT DID NOT ANSWER IT AND DID NOT ASK IT.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the three places with no joint.** Carried on the page of the twenty-fifth of August in the yard's own words: this yard does not know why the three are there and is not going to guess. They are owed a placement in a later volume as a fact and not as an identification, and they are not the nine-line mark.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what the shard, the packing and the marking on the mole's crown carry.** Not read, not identified, not settled. The two objects of nobody's have not moved and one tarpaulin still has a corner off it since the seventeenth of June, and they are about a hundred and fifty days up the crown at the close of this batch.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the coast road.** Described, published, doubted, priced in a press in a city, and **not resolved and not called anything.** Five miles of it not looked at, the Kettle not looked at, and the refused license not re-decided and not argued. **A man of the first lay has said in a market that a figure and a sentence are going to be on the same page in a city, and the two figures are still both in type and neither is withdrawn, and no chapter of this batch decided which of them is right.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers**, and the lead plug's number, and the run under this town, and the compartment, and the first echo's witness, and Neave Tarrant, nine winters, out since the twenty-eighth of February, not found, one line, and this yard has not gone.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — leaf 88.** A page in a file in a city that the copy of leaf 62 is to be read with, that nobody in this harbor has seen, that two people in this town can prove exists, and whose contents are not on a page of this book.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the not-telling of the seventh of August.** Five entries and a fifth occasion not to, and it is not paid and is not repaired and is not going to be paid in this volume.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the rest of a listening buoy.** The book of hours is the first thing and is paid for. **The stage, and its costs of maintenance, public exposure and dependence on a trained crew, are not discharged, and the Bellwright stage is Volume 5 and requires a crew.**

## Volume 04, Batch 0005 (Chapters 191–200) — as written, Movement 5, the public copy record. THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX. **THE VOLUME IS NOT CLOSED.**

**41,631 words across ten chapters, WHICH IS THE FIGURE `reviews/volume-04/volume-04-close.md` § 21a RE-MEASURED AFTER ITS NINETEEN MECHANICAL REPAIRS, IN PLACE OF THE 41,590 THIS SECTION GAVE, WHICH IS WITHDRAWN AND NAMED AS WITHDRAWN HERE RATHER THAN SILENTLY REPLACED** — 41,571 before that batch's own review repairs, which are disposed of in `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0005 (Volume 04) review repairs* — Monday the sixteenth of November to Saturday the fifth of December 2026, measured with `wc -w` including headings, against the 2,200–3,200 guideline. **TWO CHAPTERS ARE INSIDE THE GUIDELINE AND EIGHT ARE PAST IT, AND THE FIGURES ARE ON THE RECORD AND ARE NOT EXCUSED, AS RE-MEASURED: 196 5,954 · 200 4,943 · 199 4,931 · 192 4,284 · 197 4,291 · 198 3,902 · 195 3,719 · 194 3,713 · 191 2,965 · 193 2,929.** The full accounting is `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0005 as written (Chapters 191–200), Volume 04*; the duplication audit is its § 10 and the shape of the batch is its § 11. **THE SIX OPEN THREADS ARE THE SIX OPEN THREADS AND THIS BATCH OPENED NO SEVENTH. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED, NO SCENE WAS CUT, NO COST WAS DROPPED AND NO PLANNED PLOT WAS ALTERED.**

### Carried and paid

- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE PROVISIONAL PUBLIC COPY RECORD, WHICH IS A COPY OF A RECORD AND NOT THE OBJECT AND NOT A FRAGMENT.** Made at the counter of the copying room of the Morrow circuit office on Tuesday the twenty-fourth of November on an application the party made in its own left hand on Monday the sixteenth at a cost of a penny in the ordinary post, out of a form of the twenty-third that said a party may apply and that **no date attaches**, and whose foot carried the name of an office and no man's name. **SIX HEADS AND ABOUT A PAGE AND A HALF, AND IT CARRIES ON ITS FACE THE LIMITS THE RECORD ITSELF CARRIES, PRINTED IN THE RECORD'S OWN WORDS AND NOT A CLERK'S. IT SHOWS THAT A MANIFEST WAS ALTERED. IT SAYS ON ITS OWN FACE THAT IT DOES NOT SHOW BY WHOSE HAND, AT WHOSE INSTANCE OR WITH WHAT INTENT, AND NAMES NO PERSON. THE PARTY SAID BOTH HALVES IN THE FIRST COLUMN ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH WITH THE REASON AGAINST THE LINE, AND ENTERED THAT NEITHER HALF IS A DISCOVERY IN THIS YARD'S BECAUSE BOTH HAVE BEEN ON ITS PAGE SINCE JUNE AND AGAIN ON THE TENTH OF NOVEMBER, AND THAT A COPY OF A RECORD IS A THING NOBODY IN THIS HARBOR CAN CHECK WAS THE PARTY'S OWN SENTENCE IN JULY AND THAT APPLYING FOR ONE HAS NOT IMPROVED IT AND IS NOT GOING TO BE IMPROVED ON BY ANYTHING SAID IN A ROOM.** WHO ALTERED IT IS NOT SHOWN AND MAY NOT BE SHOWN IN THIS VOLUME, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH STATES IT, GUESSES AT IT, OR ADDS A GROUND THAT IDENTIFIES A PERSON.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE SITTING, AND THE COST OF IT, WHICH IS THE PARTY'S OWN ACCOUNT OF THE PARTY'S OWN EARS.** In the hearing room over the tide-gang store on Thursday the twenty-sixth and Friday the twenty-seventh, about eleven people and about nine, the store shut behind them and a man of the tide gang a morning down. **THE COPY WAS READ OUT WHOLE BY THE SHIP'S HAND OF THE PARTY AND NOT BY THE HOLDER, AND THE CLERK ENTERED THAT THE ANSWER CAME FROM HER, WHICH IS THE SECOND TIME THAT HAS BEEN ENTERED IN THAT ROOM. THE HOLDER WAS THEN ASKED, BY A CLERK WHO HAD WRITTEN THE QUESTION IN HIS OWN PAGE FIRST, WHETHER HE COULD SAY WHAT WAS IN THE WATER OFF THE SOUTH END OF THAT MOLE ON ANY DAY THAT YEAR. HE SAID HE COULD GIVE A FIGURE, AN HOUR, A STATE OF THE TIDE AND THE NAME OF THE MAN WHO HAULED THE LINE, AND THAT HE CANNOT TELL THE ROOM WHAT WAS UNDER THAT WATER AND NEVER HAS BEEN ABLE TO, AND THAT HE SAID A THING IN A MARKET ON THE EIGHTEENTH OF JULY THAT WAS WRONG IN THREE PARTS OUT OF FOUR AND HAS NOT TAKEN IT BACK AND IS NOT GOING TO BE ASKED TO AND IS NOT REFUSING TO BE ASKED TO, AND THAT THIS YARD IS NOT GOING TO ASK THAT OFFICE FOR TWO ROOMS, AND THAT HE CANNOT VOUCH FOR THE WATER WITH HIS OWN EARS AND THE BEST READER OF JOINS ON THIS COAST CAN TELL THAT TWO THINGS WERE NOT MADE AT ONE TIME AND CANNOT TELL WHICH OF THE TWO IS THE TRUE ONE, AND THAT THAT IS A THING ABOUT A MAN AND NOT ABOUT A TRADE, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO GET OUT OF IT BY BEING RIGHT ABOUT SOMETHING.** The clerk entered all of it and nobody in the room said anything. **THE REASON THE RECORD OF THE TWENTY-NINTH OF OCTOBER WAS READ BACK TO HIM IS THAT IT IS FOUR LINES ABOVE THE QUESTION AND THE PARTY IS NOT ASKING FOR TWO ROOMS. NO SECOND SEAM WAS PUT IN THIS BATCH, A SEARCH OF THE TEN CHAPTERS FOR *SEAM* RETURNS ONLY THE PHRASE *THE FIFTH SEAM*, AND THE READING OF THE CREW'S BOOK IN A ROOM IS NOT REPEATED.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE OBJECT STAYS IN REGISTRY CUSTODY AND THE HOLDER IS THE ONE WHO ACCEPTS IT, IN A ROOM AND NOT IN A SHED, AND IT IS ENTERED.** The clerk put the question in his own words: does the party apply for the item. **The answer was no, in about four minutes, with the whole of the reason, and the clerk entered that the item stays in the custody of that office and that this party accepts that in that room in front of a clerk on that date and is not going to be told it was sorry about it in a street. The reason is not a reason about the item: it is that the only road to that item on this coast runs through a court that has not sat, and every office that could put a person in front of it would have to ask this party something it entered on the sixteenth, which is that it does not put a name on a thing it cannot check. AND HE SAID IN THAT ROOM THAT IF THE ITEM CAME UP THIS HILL THAT AFTERNOON IT WOULD BE WORTH MORE TO THIS YARD THAN EVERY MARK THAT HAS GONE THROUGH ITS HANDS SINCE THE FIRST OF MAY AND THAT HE WOULD NOT KNOW WHAT TO DO WITH IT, AND THAT FOUR PEOPLE OF THIS COAST WROTE A LINE IN OCTOBER ABOUT WHAT GETS SAID AND WHAT DOES NOT, AND THAT THIS YARD IS NOT GOING TO USE A PIECE OF GLASS TO BE THE REASON IT DOES NOT ASK.** The shape of sentence was said once in that room and improved on by nobody and no character remarked on how often it has been said.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE CROWN'S ACKNOWLEDGMENT IN A FORM THAT GOES ON A PAGE, READ OUT IN A MARKET IN THIS HARBOR, NOT BY AN OFFICER.** The circuit office acknowledged the discrepancy in a form of half a sheet dated Sunday the twenty-ninth, with no signature anywhere and the name of an office at the foot, and put its reason on the face of it: **a copy which has been made cannot be unmade, and an office which has released a transcript containing a discrepancy and has not acknowledged it will be read in four years as an office which released it.** **Pernel Saye, fifty-eight, a copyist, took it up in the market on the Monday of her own account and unasked, said a sheet of that office is a public sheet and is the only kind worth the candle, said in thirty-one years of copying she has never copied one of that office's for nothing and is not going to start, said that the fourth paragraph is the part that was put in on purpose, made twenty-one copies at a penny a sheet in about nine minutes a sheet, and read the whole form out at the head of the fish dock on Tuesday the first of December in about forty people's hearing in about nine minutes. SHE WAS NOT PAID BY THIS YARD AND THIS YARD DID NOT ASK HER, DID NOT SEND ANYBODY AND DID NOT STAND NEAR HER WHILE SHE DID IT, AND SHE SAID SHE WOULD RATHER THEY DID NOT COME AND STAND BY HER. THE COST OF IT IS IN THE COPYIST'S TRADE AND IN THE MARKET'S AND NOT ONE PENNY OF IT CAME OUT OF THIS YARD. A MAN OF THE FIRST LAY BOUGHT FOUR COPIES AT FOUR PENCE; TWO MEN OF THE THIRD LAY READ THE WHOLE OF IT STANDING UP AND GAVE IT BACK WITHOUT BUYING A SHEET. THE PHRASE ON THE PAGE IS THE OFFICE'S AND NOT THE YARD'S AND THE YARD STOOD IN THAT MARKET AND LET A FORM ABOUT ITSELF BE READ OUT.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION, OPENED IN A MARKET ON THE SECOND OF DECEMBER AND NOT ANSWERED.** A man of the first lay, fifty-one, master of a coaster of about a hundred and ninety tons of his own, asked two questions in about forty people's hearing. The first was which office agreed to an alteration, and it was refused in about four minutes. **THE SECOND WAS WHAT AN OFFICE IN THIS COUNTRY THAT HAS KEPT RECORDS FOR THREE HUNDRED YEARS THOUGHT IT WAS GUARDING AGAINST AT THE POINT AT WHICH IT DECIDED WHAT IT WAS ALLOWED TO LOSE, AND IT WAS NOT ANSWERED. THE ANSWER WAS THAT THIS YARD DOES NOT KNOW, GIVEN WITH THE REASON OF THE THIRTEENTH OF AUGUST, AND THE YARD SAID IT IS NOT GOING TO SAY IT A SECOND TIME ABOUT THE SAME THING, AND IT THEN GAVE OUT OF ITS OWN BOOK THE BARE SECOND-COLUMN FACT OF THE FIFTEENTH OF AUGUST AND SAID IT WAS NAMING NOBODY AND SAYING NOTHING WHATEVER ANYBODY THOUGHT THEY WERE PROTECTING AND THAT IT WAS NOT GOING TO CALL A FACT AN ANSWER. NO OFFICE IN VEYR IS SHOWN A DOCUMENT THAT WOULD ANSWER IT, NO COMPACT IS NAMED, NO MINISTRY IS NAMED, AND NO PERSON ANSWERS IT IN A ROOM. THE PHRASE *RISK MANAGEMENT* IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE CONCRETE RESOLUTION'S REMAINING SIX ITEMS, IN THE OUTLINE'S ORDER, EACH PAID BY SAYING IT OUT LOUD RATHER THAN BY WINNING ANYTHING.** A boy off the stone and not replaced with a first-column line and a date on it and the three nots standing; **a chandler's note that is not re-written and was said out loud at the head of this dock on the third of December in about eleven people's hearing for the third time in seven months, with the three counts unmoved at twenty-two and thirteen and eleven, and the yard said it is not going to say it a fourth time**; a longer leash with a name on it, and the name is an office and not a man this time; a reeve who has nothing left to put in his book and is not a friend and said so himself; **five bells at about five and a half fathoms that are not raised and are not on the schedule of this harbor authority, nor of the circuit office, nor of anybody's, and whose four people hold a limit the yard has not asked twice to have lifted and did not ask in twenty-one days**; and the market question, not answered, with the refusal on the page in the yard's own hand with the reason against it, and the reason is about a person of no office and not about the office.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE RELATIONSHIP CHANGE, WHICH IS AN ASK AND A PRICE AND A NO, AND THE NO IS NOT A PUNISHMENT AND NOT A REPAIR.** See *Opened or advanced by this batch*, item 1.

### Opened or advanced by this batch

- **NEW, AND PAID — HE ASKED HER FOR SOMETHING AND SHE PRICED IT BEFORE SHE ANSWERED IT AND THE ANSWER WAS NO.** He wrote to her on the sixteenth of November, one question, in his own left hand, dated, and the question was **whether this party might offer her a part payment on the forty marks out of a day of work and take the rest when there is any.** It asked for nothing else and said that if the answer is no then it is no and the yard will enter it with the date and will not ask a second time. **She answered on the twenty-seventh, in her own hand, one sheet, price first and answer second, and the price is that the letter be kept and what she has written go in the second column with a reason against the line and not one word of it go in a market; and her reason is that in about four years a man in a city is going to read that a yard of Morrow Quay went to a woman it owed forty marks to and asked whether it might pay her part of it and was refused, and that the said reading is going to be true of a man like him and is not going to be true of him, and that there is nothing she can write from where she sits that will make the difference. THE ANSWER IS NO, on the ground that a part payment is a receipt and a receipt is a document and a document is how a person of no office becomes a thing a person with an office can use.** She also wrote that the six marks and two days has not moved and is not going to be reduced by anybody including by her, because it is her price and not a debt and the two have never been one thing, and that this party asked her once in twenty days and is not to ask again this winter, the reason being that a price is a price and he asked for one. **The forty marks stands first after food, unpaid since the fifteenth of October, not one mark of it paid, not one shilling of it offered, and a part payment asked about once and refused in writing once. THE LETTER SAYS NOT ONE WORD ABOUT THE COPY, THE ROOM, THE SITTING OR THE SHEET, AND THE YARD DID NOT TELL HER ABOUT ANY OF THEM AND IS NOT GOING TO. THE NOT-TELLING OF THE SEVENTH OF AUGUST IS A SIXTH OCCASION NOT TO, ON A LINE OF ITS OWN, WITH THE REASON OF THE FOURTH OF OCTOBER GIVEN AGAIN AND THE UNDERTAKING NOT TO INVENT A SECOND ONE, AND IT IS NOT ON THE SAME LINE AS THE ANSWER AND IS NOT PAID, AND A PRICE IS NOT A CONFESSION AND A REFUSAL IS NOT A REPAIR.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE ELIGIBILITY FOR THE FULL SECOND BELL EXAMINATION IS GONE, AND IT IS NOT THE WRIT, AND THE WRIT IS EXPRESSLY NOT IT.** A form of half a sheet came up this hill in the ordinary post on Friday the fourth of December with the name of a court in a city at the head and the name of that court at the foot and no signature anywhere. **It says that the matter of the ASTER GANNET stands before that court and is not closed, and that a person entered in it as an interested party is not examined for the full Second Bell license while it stands; that the holder of the writ is so entered BY THE MAKING OF A COPY OF A RECORD, the entry being on the face of that copy, and the entry is dated the twenty-fourth day of November instant, which is the day the copy came off the counter, in consequence of an application this party made on the sixteenth at a cost of a penny; that he was put to the examination once before and did not satisfy it, and that that court has before it no other matter concerning him and knows nothing whatever of his trade; THAT IT IS NOT A SUSPENSION, NOT A PENALTY AND NOT A FINDING AGAINST ANYBODY, AND THAT A PERSON WHO IS NOT EXAMINED IS NOT A PERSON WHO HAS BEEN REFUSED; and that the writ of the first of May is not before that court, is not a Second Bell license, does not lead to one and renews nothing, that the court has not considered it and takes no notice of it, and that nothing in the paper touches, grants, refuses or deals with the second season of that writ. A COPY OF THE SAID RECORD IS TO BE PRODUCED TO THAT COURT ON THE FOURTH DAY OF JANUARY, AND A PARTY WHICH DOES NOT PRODUCE IT IS NOT FINED, BECAUSE A COURT DOES NOT FINE A POOR MAN, AND THE MATTER SIMPLY GOES ON.** The holder entered it in the first column in his own left hand and before any figure: that he was put to the examination in the September of two years back and did not satisfy it, that the writ was granted eight months afterwards, that he has not gone near it since, and that this yard is not going to say in a street in January that the writ did this. **THE WHOLE OF THE RELEASE WAS THE PARTY'S OWN DOING AND THE PARTY IS NOT GOING TO CALL THE COST SOMEBODY ELSE'S. NO LATER CHAPTER MAY MOVE THIS ONTO THE WRIT, MAY SAY THE COURT REFUSED HIM ANYTHING, MAY SAY THE COURT FOUND AGAINST HIM, OR MAY SAY HE HAS SAT THE EXAMINATION SINCE THE SEPTEMBER OF TWO YEARS AGO.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A REEVE'S LEDGER SPENT AND A ROOM AND A DAY, AND THE PRICE FELL ON A MAN OF THE TIDE GANG.** On the nineteenth of November the holder asked the officer of the harbor authority, in the open at the foot of the mole's steps in about eleven people's hearing, for a room and a day and **NOT FOR AN INSTRUMENT**, saying that there are eight acknowledgements in that ledger and the ninth was entered on the twenty-eighth of August at this party's asking and is spent and a tenth may not be entered and was not asked for, and that a room is not an instrument and a day is not an instrument, and that he was not asking that office to seat anybody, **that the power to seat one of two disputants belongs to the inquiry and not to the writ, and that the two instruments are not the same instrument.** The reeve named the price before he said yes: the room is over the tide-gang store, a sitting in it shuts the store, and one man of the tide gang of this harbor loses his Thursday morning, and the tide gang is four men short and has been since before he came to the office and will be four men short at the first of March, and he did not name the man and gave the reason, that the moment he names him he is a man in this business. **The price did not fall on the third lay's four days and not on a man of the first lay's Monday and not on Fenn Ardle's Tuesday.** He said three further things: that his ledger is spent and no line he can enter this week would mean anything to anybody in the first of March; that his office cannot pay for a clerk's day, has had nothing in its repair account since the middle of February, and is going to owe its clerk a day, and the debt is entered against the office and not against the party; and **that he is not a friend of this party, has never said he was, is not going to begin on a Thursday in November, and that if the party needs a man in this harbor to be on its side then it has counted him twice and should take one of them out.** The holder entered all of it and said the party takes none of them out and agrees with him.
- **NEW, AND PAID — AN ELEVEN-LINE LIST OF WHAT IS NOT GOING INTO THE ROOM, WRITTEN ON A SUNDAY, READ BACK TWICE BY A CLERK WHO ASKED ABOUT NONE OF IT.** The place of the case of five bells; the four people it belongs to; the limit they wrote themselves; the plate at the fifth seam; the two objects on the crown of the mole; the boy; the forty marks standing whole to him; the forty marks first after food to a person of no office; the delay; a letter expected in the ordinary post; and the two figures for the bar of gravel together with the Kettle, which was not looked at. **The holder stopped the woman at the ninth item and made a tenth of it, which is the letter, and the reason is that a line about a person of no office in a room is a document about her. THIS IS THE BATCH'S ONE WORKING RULE AND IT IS NOT A DISCOVERY: the reason against a line is the only part of it that would keep four people safe and a reason does not go into a record, which the yard worked out on the thirtieth of October and did not work out again here.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE HARBOR'S POVERTY AT THE END OF MOVEMENT 5, AND IT IS WORSE.** A bag of four marks and six shillings and five pence against a mark and eight shillings and sixpence twenty days earlier, **the whole of the difference being two days of work, two shillings to a bonesetter, one wage and a penny of postage**; two wages of four marks not paid and **six weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them, the fifteenth of August, the fifth of September, the sixth of October, the third of November, the seventeenth of November and the first of December, all six given in the same breath wherever the figure appears**; a morning down for a man of the tide gang; a charge of twenty-one pence for a strapping coming off and a quarter of a year out of the left side of a shipwright; one herring berth of two out; the north berth out; the light not on; the tide gang four short; a hard at Ashtide unanswered; a fire party's hose dead; nineteen winters in a list in a city; the *Sea Wren* at the pile with this party not having thanked him; **and the cistern behind the engine house charged on six rota days in three weeks at no figure and walked on none of them, and the charge of the ninth of November standing entered with no figure against it, and the party on the record as not going to say what six charges and that one make between them.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A PARTY THAT HAS SEEN A DOCUMENT AND SAID NOTHING ABOUT IT IN A MARKET, ENTERED AGAINST ITSELF.** The holder entered on the twenty-fifth and again on the fourth of December that from the twenty-fourth of November this yard is a party that has seen a document and said nothing about it in a market, and that there is no version of this yard after that day in which that is not a fact about it, and that he entered it against himself and is not going to be thanked for it and is not asking. **The reason is in the second column and it is about the four people of this coast the yard did not tell about.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A FORM OF THE COURT WHICH THIS YARD HAS NOT SHOWN TO ANYBODY.** The holder entered in the second column of the fourth of December that the form of the fourth has not been shown to the clerk of the harbor authority, to the market, to the copyist of this harbor or to anybody in this town, that the only other person in this harbor who has read it is the ship's hand, and that the reason is not a secret and is that this yard is not going to be the thing that puts a date out of a city in front of four hundred people, and that the form will be produced to any office that asks for it in a form and that none has. **THE REASON AGAINST THE LINE IS THAT WHETHER THAT IS A PIECE OF QUIET OR A PIECE OF HIDING IS NOT CHECKABLE FROM A MOLE, AND THIS YARD DOES NOT KNOW.**

### Ruled

- **RULED — NO POWER STAGE OF ANY KIND WAS TAKEN AND NONE MAY BE.** The Faultreader stage is spent, is not re-taken, is not improved, and **the word is on no page of these ten chapters.** **No person of this yard was in any water on any day of this batch, no finding of worked glass was made and nothing was raised.** The book of hours stands at sixteen lines, was not extended, and its costs of maintenance, public exposure and dependence on a trained crew are NOT discharged and were not touched.
- **RULED — THE CONDITION IS NAMED NOWHERE IN THIS BATCH.** It is carried once, in the second column of Chapter 195, as a bare fact with a reason against it and without a name. **The four lines above the named line of the twentieth of July are still four, are still nameless, are not struck out, and a request to strike one out of a column is refused in the same words it is refused for a line in any column.**
- **RULED — THE BELL IS NOT AN INSTRUMENT AND WAS NOT TOUCHED.** No boat went off the south end, no second eight marks was entered as paid, the bellwright was not asked, he is in no chapter of this batch as a person, no digit of anything he said on the second of July is printed, and the chest was not opened and was not brought down the hill. **The standing sentence said in a street on the third of May is quoted in Chapter 200's closing position in its own terms and stands unreversed.**
- **RULED — THE LEAD PLUG IS UNTOUCHED.** Not asked about, not printed, not opened, and not brought down the hill.
- **RULED — THE CUSTODY ITEMS ARE UNTOUCHED.** Three items, three numbers, **no digit quoted in any of the ten chapters**, none returns, none is asked for, the custody room is not asked for, and the municipal claim on the third stands. **THE WORD *CUSTODY* DOES OCCUR IN THIS BATCH AND IT HAD TO: the movement's subject is a record about an item entered in a custody room and the copy's own face says so. NO DIGIT IS QUOTED AND THE ROOM IS NOT ASKED FOR AND THE LIVE QUESTION — what a municipal claim does to a presumed Crown material — IS NOT ANSWERED AND IS NOT PUT TO ANYBODY IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.**
- **RULED — NO RETURN WAS MADE AND THERE IS NO FIFTH.** A finding of worked glass was made on no day of this batch, nothing was raised on any day of it, the reporting clause was not touched, and no day was reported to any office.
- **RULED — NO PERSON, BOAT OR LEAD OF THIS YARD WENT INSIDE THE FIRST CLAUSE OF THE NOTICE OF THE THIRTY-FIRST ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH.** The plate at the fifth seam was not reached, not measured, not touched and not asked about, the eight-clause plan of the seventeenth of January was not reopened, no better letter was written about it in three weeks, and **THE PLATE NOTE IS OPEN AND IS NOT CLOSED IN THIS BATCH AND IS NOT REQUIRED TO BE, because the document that would close it is spent.**
- **RULED — NO SHEET WENT UP ON ANY WALL IN THIS HARBOR IN THE THREE WEEKS ENDED ON THE FIFTH OF DECEMBER AND NONE CAME OFF, and that is entered in the first column of Chapter 200, and the volume's final chapter image is on no wall and was not attempted.**
- **RULED — THE MAN IN A COAT IS NOT NAMED AND IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH. THE COMMISSIONED OFFICER IS NOT NAMED. HALVEN ROOK IS NOT IN A ROOM. MAGISTER ILYA SERR IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH AT ALL. CAPTAIN VEYRA HOLT IS NOT IN A ROOM AND NOT IN THIS HARBOR AND IS A FACT ON A PAGE.**
- **RULED — AMBROSE SELL IS NAMED WHEREVER A CLERK OF THE CIRCUIT OFFICE APPEARS**, in Chapters 194 and 196, and **he said neither of the two things he said in September and in October a third time, he did not name the magistrate, and no person in this batch learned the name of the man in a coat.**
- **RULED — NO NEW ORDINARY PERSON WAS CREATED IN THIS BATCH.** Every person in these ten chapters was already named in this volume, and the list is in `state/current.md` § *The relationship pressure at the end of Chapter 200*. **Pernel Saye, Wil Tranter and Denzil Hare all existed before Monday the sixteenth of November.**
- **RULED — ALDEN MARR IS AT THE FAR BENCH ON FOUR DAYS OF THIS BATCH, BEING THE SIXTEENTH AND THE NINETEENTH OF NOVEMBER, THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF NOVEMBER, AND THE THIRD OF DECEMBER, AND ON NO OTHER DAY OF THE TWENTY-ONE,** not asked for anything on any of the four and asking nothing on any of the four, and Calder Marr did not go over to him on any of the four. **THE FIGURE OF FOUR IS COUNTED FROM THE PROSE AND WRITTEN FROM THE COUNT, WHICH IS THE RULE THE BATCH 0002 REVIEW LEFT AFTER TWO FAR-BENCH FIGURES HAD TO BE CORRECTED AGAINST THE CHAPTERS.**
- **RULED — THE READ-OUT DEVICE WAS USED ONCE IN THIS BATCH, at the head of this dock on the evening of Friday the fourth of December, in about eleven people's hearing in about nine minutes, and not one line of the second column was read out in it. THE COPYIST'S READING OF AN OFFICE'S FORM IN A MARKET ON THE FIRST OF DECEMBER IS NOT THIS DEVICE AND THE ENTRY OF THAT CHAPTER SAYS SO ON ITS FACE. THE READING OF THE CREW'S BOOK IN CHAPTER 196 IS NOT A USE OF IT AND IS NOT A READING OF THE BOOK.** The read-back runs in four of the ten chapters, 191, 193, 195 and 200. **THE FIGURE OF THREE, BEING 191, 195 AND 200, IS WITHDRAWN AND NOT SILENTLY REPLACED: `chapter-0193.md:17` carries it, the slate going into the book in the ship's own writing and the holder reading it back after her, and a plain search of the ten chapters for *read it back*, *read them back* and *read back after her* returns 191, 193, 195 and 200 and nothing else.** The clerk reading an entry back in a doorway or a room in Chapters 194 and 196 is his own trade's practice and is not the shed device.
- **RULED — THE PHRASE *RISK MANAGEMENT* IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH, AND THE PAGE THAT WENT OUT OF A FILE IS NOT PRODUCED, DESCRIBED, SUMMARISED OR REPEATED AS A DISCOVERY OR AS A SCENE.**
- **RULED — THE WORD *HARBORS* IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH, INCLUDING INSIDE ANY OTHER WORD, AND A CASE-INSENSITIVE SEARCH OF THE TEN CHAPTERS FOR *Nine Harbors*, *Quietening*, *Crown Seal*, *Office of Returning Memory*, *Orison* AND ALL NINE ROUTE NAMES RETURNS NOTHING.**
- **RULED — THE SLATE WALL'S COUNTER DID NOT RESTART AND IS TWENTY-EIGHT, TWENTY-NINE AND THIRTY** on the sixteenth, the twenty-third and the thirtieth of November, all bare, all with the clerk keeping his own page, and no sitting held on any of them.
- **RULED — THE GRID. THIS BATCH TAKES ITS DAYS FROM CHAPTER 190 AND FROM ITS OWN CHAPTERS, AS LOCK L1 AS AMENDED BINDS.** The Mondays are the sixteenth, the twenty-third and the thirtieth of November; the wage Tuesdays are the seventeenth and the twenty-fourth of November and the first of December; the rota is a Monday and a Thursday, so the rota days are the nineteenth and twenty-sixth of November and the third of December; the slate wall is wiped on every Monday and the counter is the chapters' counter, which does not restart and has the ninth of November at twenty-seven. **NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH RESTATED A WEEKDAY FOR ANY DAY BEFORE THE ELEVENTH OF NOVEMBER, NO CHAPTER STATED THE YEAR, AND NOT ONE DAY OF CHAPTERS 151 TO 190 WAS MOVED.**
- **RULED — THE MARKET WALL AND THE HEARING ROOM ARE NEVER THE SAME DAY, VERIFIED A SIXTH TIME.** The hearing room was used on the twenty-sixth and twenty-seventh of November; the market was used on the first, the second and the fourth of December, and a copyist's counter off the fish market on the thirtieth of November and the first.

### Open by design

- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the answer inside the delayed note.** Eleven logged occasions and two of them with no call in them, **neither advanced in this batch by arithmetic or by a measurement, no occasion entered, no measurement taken**, the delay a hand's width of a second and unmoved, and nobody in this yard and nobody in this harbor has said why. **THE ANSWER IS RESERVED TO THE VOLUME 15 MIDPOINT AND THE VOLUME'S FINAL CHAPTER IMAGE, WHICH INCLUDES THE DELAY BEING WRITTEN DOWN WITH A DATE AGAINST IT, IS NOT REACHED BY THIS BATCH AND WAS NOT ATTEMPTED.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — who altered the manifest.** The copy says it does not show and names no person, and the party says the same in the first column. **It may not be shown in this volume, and no chapter of this batch guessed at it.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — who drafted the fourth paragraph of the notice of the tenth of September.** Carried. Nobody in this batch remarked on it and nobody asked it.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the three places with no joint.** Carried on the page of the twenty-fifth of August in the yard's own words. This yard does not know why the three are there and is not going to guess, and the bare fact of the second column of the second of December says the same thing in one line.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what the shard, the packing and the marking on the mole's crown carry.** Not read, not identified, not settled. The two objects of nobody's are about a hundred and seventy-five days up the crown at the close of this batch and neither has moved.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the coast road.** Described, published, doubted, priced in a press in a city, and **not resolved and not called anything.** Neither figure is withdrawn, the Kettle was not looked at, and the refused licence was not re-decided. **The two figures went onto the eleven-line list of what is not going into the room.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers**, and the lead plug's number, and the run under this town, and the compartment, and the first echo's witness, and Neave Tarrant, nine winters, out since the twenty-eighth of February, not found, one line, and this yard has not gone. **The plate note at the fifth seam is open and is not closed, because the document that would close it is spent.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — leaf 88.** A page in a file in a city that nobody in this harbor has seen, whose contents are not on a page of this book and were not asked for in this batch.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the not-telling of the seventh of August.** **Six entries and a sixth occasion not to, entered on the sixteenth of November, and it is not paid, not repaired, not told as a confession, and not the price of anything she gives him.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the rest of a listening buoy.** The book of hours is sixteen lines and stands where it stood. The stage and its costs of maintenance, public exposure and dependence on a trained crew are not discharged, and the Bellwright stage is Volume 5 and requires a crew.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what an office thought it was guarding against.** **THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION. Asked in a market in this harbor on the second of December in about forty people's hearing and not answered, and the party's answer is that it does not know, and it is going to be asked again by somebody and the yard has entered that a question in a market that is not answered is a different animal from a question that is never asked.**

### What the Volume 04 close inherits, in one place

1. **THE VOLUME'S FIFTY CHAPTERS ARE WRITTEN AND THE VOLUME'S FINAL CHAPTER IMAGE IS NOT PAID.** `outline/volume-04.md` gives Movement 5 the range 191–200 and this batch carries it to 200, **and the outline's § *Final chapter image* is not reached and was not attempted, and the dispatch for this batch said in terms that the volume ends inside this movement and that the volume close is a later phase and is to be told by whoever writes it.** The close phase therefore exists and is queued at `workspace/volume-04/close/PROMPT.md`, and it is the only phase created by this batch.
2. **WHAT IS ON THE TABLE FOR THE CLOSE, in the outline's order and all of it unpaid on the page:** a page going up on a market wall where the two naval notices used to be, and **no sheet went up on any wall in three weeks and none came off, and that is entered**; a person going out in a boat because the whole of the craft is the going out, **and the standing sentence of the third of May is quoted in Chapter 200's closing position and stands unreversed**; the boy off the stone and the third column with a name in it that is not the boy's and is not the holder's, **and the third column is empty, has not been filled since the first of August, and nobody has been appointed to it**; and the delay written down anyway with a date against it, **and it was not measured, not advanced and not written down.**
3. **THE STATE OF EVERY FIGURE AT THE CLOSE, all of it checkable in pence:** four marks and six shillings and five pence in the bag, being 717p, against 262p on the tenth of November; twenty-six lines and one hundred and four marks in the wage column with the fifth of March the oldest; twenty envelopes on a bench; **six weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them, being the fifteenth of August, the fifth of September, the sixth of October, the third of November, the seventeenth of November and the first of December, and all six are to be given in the same breath wherever the figure appears**; thirty-one on the chandler's note, not re-written, its three counts unmoved at twenty-two, thirteen and eleven; nothing in a tin since the twenty-eighth of April; three marks owing to a man of Ashtide for the eighth of August; seven pence to this yard's own shipwright; **eight marks and a day of a boat to this harbor's own bellwright since the twenty-third of May, not asked about, the chest not opened**; eight shillings and tenpence to a woman of this harbor; forty marks standing whole to a boy of twenty-two; forty marks to a person of no office, first after food, unpaid since the fifteenth of October.
4. **THE TWO DATES IN A CITY THAT THE CLOSE OWES A LOOK AT AND NOT AN ANSWER:** a copy of a record is to be produced to the Court of Wreck Claims on the fourth day of January, and the writ of the first of May runs to midnight on the first of March with the second season a named absence and the harbor authority not the office that can renew it. **This yard has not asked any office for anything in aid of the January date, has not sent to a city, has not written to a man in a coat whose name is on no page of this book, and has not been to the counter of that circuit office since the twenty-fourth of November.**
5. **THE RELATIONSHIP POSITION, WHICH THE CLOSE MAY NOT REPAIR:** he asked her for something, she priced it, the answer is no, the forty marks stands first after food, the six marks and two days has not moved, and **the not-telling of the seventh of August is six entries and is not paid.** Neither of them gave the other anything and neither is to be repaired by either of them giving the other anything.
6. **THE INSTRUMENT THE VOLUME LEAVES THE NEXT WRITER, which is the second column and not the first:** eleven things that stay in the shed, a bare fact about places kept by more than one community, a reason that does not travel, a page and a half that nobody in this harbor can check, and a form out of a court that this yard has not shown to anybody. **THE FIRST COLUMN IS CHECKABLE AND THE SECOND IS NOT, AND THE WHOLE OF THIS VOLUME IS THE DIFFERENCE, AND THE CLOSE MAY NOT CLOSE THAT DIFFERENCE.**
7. **THE SIX OPEN THREADS AND THE THIRTEEN CARRIED ALONGSIDE THEM, unchanged, and the one queued phase is `workspace/volume-04/close/PROMPT.md` and nothing beyond it exists.**
8. **AND IT IS NOT A SEVENTH OPEN THREAD, BUT IT IS A CHECKABLE INCONSISTENCY THE CLOSE SHOULD RULE ON, SO IT IS NAMED HERE RATHER THAN LEFT IN A CHAPTER: THREE CHAPTERS GIVE THREE STATES OF THE MARKET WALL. `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0150.md` stages the sheet of the fifteenth of August going up on the OUTSIDE of the naval paper of the fourth of August and has the ship's hand say so in about forty people's hearing; `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0174.md:43` has a man call it a sheet under the naval paper that came down in August; `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0192.md:19` says a naval paper was nailed over it. THEY CANNOT ALL BE TRUE. None of the three is in this batch, none was edited, and the Batch 0005 review's finding that Chapter 199 sent forty people to read a sheet Chapter 192 says was covered was repaired WITHOUT choosing between them, because Chapter 199 now repeats Chapter 192's own fact and does not say which paper is on top of which. The full flag is `state/continuity.md` § *Batch 0005 (Volume 04) review repairs* § F, and the ruling belongs to the close and should be entered on the face of both chapters.**

The volume's central pressure is resolved in a page and a half that nobody in this harbor can check, Movement 2's turn is landed, Movement 3's turn is landed and spent, Movement 4's reversal is landed and paid, and Movement 5's climax is landed and paid as a copy and not as an object.
