### Flagged for the controller, not fixable from a writer or repair phase

**THE `NOVEL_SPEC.md` § Status OWNERSHIP GAP, WHICH NO PROMPT CREATED, AND WHICH IS NAMED HERE SO THAT THE CLOSE AUDIT IS NOT THE THING THAT FINDS IT.** `NOVEL_SPEC.md` is a spec file and not one of the four state files, so no phase prompt was ever told to update it, and it appeared in the queued close prompt's *Read first* list and in no produce list. **The consequence was that its Status line stood unamended from the Volume 01 close while three volumes closed around it: it still named Volume 02 Batch 0001, Chapters 51 to 60, as the next writing phase, a prompt that was retired on 26 September 2026.** It has now been corrected once, for Volume 04, by the Volume 04 Batch 0005 review pass, and the file carries an ownership line on the face of its Status section; the Volume 04 close is named there as owning it from its phase on, and the close prompt now carries it as a fifth thing it produces. **This is recorded as a flag rather than as a repair because the underlying defect is in the prompt templates, not in the file: a spec file nobody owns rots, and no writer phase can see that it is rotting.** The remedy, if the controller wants one, is a line in the volume-close prompt template making the spec file a produce item, which this close prompt now does by hand.

**`state/phase-ledger.json` IS FLAGGED IN THIS FILE AND IS NOT RESTATED HERE.** It is the authority for the ledger record, and the ledger is two hundred chapters out of date. This phase did not edit it, did not read it as evidence, and wrote no marker. **The single statement of it in `state/current.md` stands and is not added to.**


1. **THE VOLUME'S CLIMAX IS A DOCUMENT, NOT A DIVE, AND IT IS A COPY OF A RECORD AND NOT THE OBJECT.** The party has never seen it, has never asked for it, and has entered that it is not applying this month and why. **It would show that a manifest was altered and would not show who altered it, and the copy would say so on its own face, and nobody in this harbor can check it.** Nobody was convicted of anything and nothing was decided in the sitting of the twenty-eighth and twenty-ninth, so the thing in front of Movement 5 is not a verdict and not a conviction.
2. **THE VOLUME'S ARGUMENT IS NOW AGAINST IT AS WELL AS FOR IT, AND THAT IS THE POINT OF THE MIDPOINT.** A yard that has spent two volumes saying that a record a man can check is worth more than a record he cannot has come out of a room with a bundle in a city that says two things about it that it did not choose, **and two men have said the same thing out loud in two streets and they are not the same sentence and not the same man.** A man of thirty-six at the head of a dock said that a yard which has been in a room like that is a yard whose figures are worth what a clerk's are, and said first that he was not saying the yard was wrong, and the holder said the other half of it, that the figure is not withdrawn and neither is the pilot's. A man of fifty-five at a wall said he would rather have the page in a city than the wall, asked for a name and was refused in about four minutes, and the holder said he would rather have the wall and has not got a reason for that preference which is worth anything. The yard agrees with both of them. **THE READ-OUT AT THE HEAD OF THIS DOCK ON THE TENTH OF NOVEMBER NAMES THE SECOND OF THOSE TWO AND NOT THE FIRST, AND IT DID NOT UNTIL THE BATCH 0004 REVIEW CORRECTED IT, AND A WRITER WHO INHERITS IT MUST NOT PUT THE PAGE-AND-WALL SENTENCE BACK INTO THE MOUTH OF THE MAN OF THIRTY-SIX.** **Movement 5 may not make the party win, and the honest form of its victory is a document that cannot be checked coming into a room where the people who are hardest to believe are the party itself.**
3. **THE RESOLUTION IS NAMED IN THE OUTLINE AND NOT YET PAID, AND ITS FOUR PARTS ARE:** the inquiry cannot convict the yard of the wreck; the Crown is forced to acknowledge the manifest discrepancy in a form that goes on a page and is read out in a market in this harbor; **Mara Venn leaves the Registry and does not join this crew — SHE HAS LEFT THE REGISTRY, SHE HAS NOT JOINED THIS CREW, AND NEITHER IS A SACRIFICE TO THE OTHER AND NEITHER IS REPAIRED BY EITHER OF THEM GIVING THE OTHER ANYTHING, AND NEITHER MAY BE FUSED**; and **Calder Marr loses eligibility for the full Second Bell examination, and it is not the writ that takes it and the writ is expressly not it.**
4. **THE CONCRETE RESOLUTION'S SEVEN ITEMS IN THE OUTLINE'S ORDER ARE ALL STILL OWED:** a boy off the stone and not replaced and a first-column line with a date on it; a chandler's note that is either re-written or is not and is said out loud either way; a longer leash with a name on it, and the name is an office and not a man this time; a reeve who has nothing left to put in his book and is not a friend; five bells at about five and a half fathoms that are not raised and are not on anybody's schedule and whose four people hold a limit the yard has not asked twice to have lifted; and a question asked in a market about who accepted a removal, not answered, with the refusal on the page in the yard's own hand with the reason against it.
5. **THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION IS OPEN AND IS NOT ANSWERED IN MOVEMENT 4 AND MAY NOT BE ANSWERED IN MOVEMENT 5:** if an office knew a record was unsafe and removed a page, what earlier emergency justified building a monopoly on keeping records? **The page gone out of a file and the document in which an office accepted it are SPENT, and the two words are on no page of Movement 4, and Movement 5 may not spend them again.**
6. **THE THREADS THAT MOVEMENT 5 INHERITS UNCHANGED:** the six open threads; the second-column bare fact and the spent naming of the twentieth of July; the not-telling of the seventh of August, five entries and not paid; the lead plug, the bellwright and the undertaking of the second of July; the coast road, the pilot and the Kettle; the delayed note's eleven and two; the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers; the plate at the fifth seam and the refused plan of the seventeenth of January; the chart and its bell notation in a vessel's keeping; the two objects of nobody's on the mole's crown; the three places with no joint; the shard of worked glass nobody can read; the first echo's witness; Neave Tarrant; the fourth paragraph of the notice of the tenth of September; and the harbor's poverty, which is not fixed for anything and is not fixed in this batch either.
7. **THE HARBOR'S POVERTY AT THE END OF MOVEMENT 4, AND IT IS WORSE.** A mark and eight shillings and sixpence in a bag at the close against a mark and twelve shillings at the open; a wage of four not paid on the third of November; four weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them; a day of about three marks refused by the yard itself and a wharf day of about four hours for three marks; a bonesetter's bill of three shillings and sixpence and a quarter of a year out of a shipwright's left side; a man of the second lay out of a Tuesday that is in no ledger; one herring berth of two out; the north berth out; the light not on; the tide gang four short; a hard at Ashtide unanswered; a fire party's hose dead; nineteen winters in a list in a city; the *Sea Wren* at the pile with this party not having thanked him; **and the cistern behind the engine house charged twenty-four times since the third of August up to the fifth of November and walked on none of the twenty-four, and charged again on the ninth with no figure against it, and the party is on the record as not going to say whether that is twenty-five.**
8. **THE MONEY MOVEMENT 5 OPENS WITH, AND IT IS CHECKABLE IN PENCE: a mark and eight shillings and sixpence, which is two hundred and sixty-two pence.** Twenty-five lines and a hundred marks in the wage column with the fifth of March the oldest. Nineteen envelopes on a bench. Four weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them. Thirty-one on the chandler's note, not re-written, its three counts unmoved at twenty-two and thirteen and eleven. Three marks owing to a man of Ashtide. Seven pence owing to this yard's own shipwright. Eight marks and a day of a boat owing to this harbor's own bellwright. Eight shillings and tenpence owing to a woman of this harbor. Forty marks standing whole to a boy of twenty-two. Forty marks to a person of no office, first after food, unpaid since the fifteenth of October. **The writ runs to midnight on the first of March 2027 and the second season is a named absence.**
9. **THE CREW, AND WHAT IT IS AFTER FOUR BATCHES OF THIS VOLUME.** Two and a half people. One of them is a shipwright of twenty-seven with a bone cracked about two inches above the left wrist, a strapping that comes off on the twenty-first of November and not before, a quarter of a year out of the left side, a refusal of her own nine sentences twice over, and the standing shutdown. One of them is a holder of thirty-two who has been in a room and come out less believed, who has a writ and a crew and four people of the Sill whose limit he cannot put in a record, who has five entries about a thing he has not told a woman and no second reason, and who has a book of hours he is not allowed to call a trade. **A boy of twenty-two is off the stone and is not replaced and is not in any of this volume's rooms.**
10. **A REPO AND ANOTHER INJURY ARE NOT OWED, AND MOVEMENT 5 MUST NOT PAY THEM AGAIN.** `outline/volume-04.md` § *Costs this volume may not waive* is paid in full in Movement 4 and the injury, the bad day, the body's time and the tide-sickness are all on the page with figures against them.

## Volume 04 close — the audit's account, 27 September 2026

**`reviews/volume-04/volume-04-close.md` IS THE AUDIT AND IS THE AUTHORITY FOR EVERY RULING IN THIS SECTION. EVERY ITEM IS MARKED PAID, PART, CARRIED, RULED or OPEN BY DESIGN, AS THE MARKING SCHEME AT THE HEAD OF THIS FILE REQUIRES, AND A RULING IS NOT A DEFERRAL AGAIN.**

### PAID

- **The volume's central pressure — RULED RESOLVED, AND RESOLVED IN THE PUBLICATION'S CONSEQUENCES AND NOT IN A CONVICTION.** Nothing was decided, nobody was charged, and a yard which spent two volumes saying that a record a man can check is the only kind worth having came out of a room less able to be checked, by its own method, in public, with a clerk's own entry against it (Chapters 184, 186, 196).
- **The volume's midpoint — PAID AND RATIFIED AS DELIVERED, in a narrower place than the outline aimed it.** The join went on a notice of the tenth of September and not on the yard's own work; the clerk could see the two inks himself and entered the fact before the man who put the join there; *I do not know* three times and not a fourth in a different way; the market of the eighteenth of July read back to him in his own words by a stranger in a flat voice; the third column given up to a clerk because the office cannot make a boy come (Ch 184).
- **The volume's climax — PAID IN ALL FOUR OF THE OUTLINE'S ELEMENTS** (Chapters 191 to 200), and the four rulings are at `state/continuity.md` § *The Volume 04 close* and at the audit's § 12.
- **The inquiry — PAID, and it convicted the yard of nothing and charged nobody**, and the clerk entered that on the first line of the record in his own words (Ch 196).
- **The acknowledgment and the market — PAID.** A form of half a sheet dated the twenty-ninth of November with no signature and an office's name at the foot, read out in a market in about forty people's hearing in about nine minutes by a copyist of fifty-eight at a penny a sheet, **and not one penny of it came out of this yard, and the words on the board are the office's words and the yard could not have improved them by a word** (Ch 197, 198).
- **The relationship change with Mara Venn — PAID IN THE VOLUME'S OWN FORM AND NOT THE OUTLINE'S.** He asked her for something in a letter of the sixteenth of November, she priced it in a letter of the twenty-seventh, and the answer is no, and the no is not a punishment and not a repair (Ch 191, 197).
- **The Second Bell eligibility — PAID**, and the three rulings on it are at `state/current.md` § *The hand-over to Volume 05* and at the audit's § 13.2.
- **The new-volume question — PAID AS OPENED AND NOT ANSWERED**, in a market on Wednesday the second of December in about forty people's hearing, and the yard's answer was *I do not know* with the reason of the thirteenth of August and a bare second-column fact with no name in it, and *I am not going to call a fact an answer* (Ch 199).

### PART

- **Mara Venn's break with the Registry — PAID IN A DIFFERENT FORM AND NOT AS A LEAVING.** She has held no office since the fourth of January and is not resigning anything, because there is nothing to resign from (Ch 176). What the volume paid is a refusal in writing to be cited, quoted, produced or asked again; a finding unsigned at her own asking; a line priced on the twenty-ninth of September that was an undertaking and not money; a letter of the twenty-seventh of November pricing an ask and refusing it; and forty marks first after food, unpaid.
- **The plate at the fifth seam and its note — NOT PAID. CARRIED OPEN TO VOLUME 05.** The plate is in eleven feet, seen and not measured and not touched, in twenty-five chapters; the note stands open, in twenty-five chapters; no chapter reached it. **The document that would have closed it was not withheld; it was produced, quoted, described and summarised in Chapters 175, 176 and 180, where the Registry's own form's own line reads *Reason entered by this office: risk management*, and it went to Movement 3's turn and to Mara Venn's break and not to the plate. A document produced once in this series is spent.**
- **The third column — PAID AS A FINDING AND CARRIED EMPTY.** It was read out in a room by a clerk in fourteen lines in about eleven minutes, and the clerk entered that nobody was going to be asked about it afterwards and why (Ch 184). It was not filled on any day of the fifty chapters and nobody was appointed to it, and the volume closed on it empty.
- **The nine-line mark — PAID AS NOT BEING A MARK, and CLOSED IN VOLUME 5 AND NOT BEFORE.** The nine fastenings in about seven feet of each of the six joints are read out in Ch 184 as a count of fastenings and are not called a mark, and the nine places in six books are the yard's own sheet and are not the Nine Harbors.
- **The far-bench thread — PAID TWENTY-SIX TIMES AND STILL STANDING.** Twenty-six days across the fifty chapters, in five batches of 7 / 3 / 6 / 6 / 4, counted from the prose; no fragment, no report talk, no second story, no confession, and Calder did not go over to him on any of the twenty-six.
- **The four returns — PAID AND STANDING.** Four, no fifth, all four caught by the first limb by itself, the counting rule spoken out loud in a room, and the yard's own book and a copy of it in a file in a city since September, which means the enumeration now exists in two places and neither can be withdrawn by anybody.

### RULED, each dated 27 September 2026, and each in the state files

1. **The condition's naming is SPENT**, in Chapter 164 on the seventeenth of September, in the second column, in the holder's own left hand, with a reason against the line, and never by a dive. **The four nameless lines above it are still four, are still nameless, are not struck out, and a request to strike a line out of a column is refused in the same words for any column.**
2. **The delayed note stands at eleven logged occasions and two with no call in them**, neither count advanced by arithmetic or by a measurement, no occasion entered in any of the fifty chapters, the delay a hand's width of a second and unmoved, nobody in this harbor ever having said why, **and the answer reserved to the Volume 15 midpoint. Volume 03's forward permission that a Volume 04 chapter might write the figure again on a flat calm with a slate and a stopwatch was NOT EXERCISED.**
3. **The coast road: both figures in type in a city, neither withdrawn, this volume did not decide which is right, the Kettle was not looked at, the word *network* is in no mouth, the refused licence stands unreversed and the thirty marks were not paid, and Gwil Lasker's sentence of the first of November and the man of fifty-five's sentence of the fifth are two different sentences in two different mouths.** **AND THE TWO FIGURES WENT ONTO THE ELEVENTH LINE OF THE LIST OF WHAT IS NOT GOING INTO A ROOM ON THE TWENTY-SECOND OF NOVEMBER, WITH THE KETTLE.**
4. **The custody items: three, no digit on any page, none returns, none is asked for, the municipal claim on the third stands, the live question is still unanswered and was not put to anybody, and the Court of Wreck Claims' form does not answer it and may not be read as having answered it. The word *custody* now occurs in this volume where it did not in Movement 4.**
5. **The bell, the bellwright and the lead plug: the going out of the second of May is spent and not repeated, the standing sentence of the third of May stands unreversed, the bellwright was not asked in any of the fifty chapters, no digit of anything he said on the second of July is printed, the chest was not opened, the eight marks and a day of a boat stand owing since the twenty-third of May, and the undertaking of the second of July binds the yard and not the office and not the market and not the boy.**
6. **The boy, the stone and the pencil: off the stone since the fifteenth of May and not replaced, forty marks standing whole, the three nots standing, the head of the third column keeping his hand, and a column nobody fills is a column somebody else fills, and the somebody else is a clerk with a pen, and the day he fills it is the day this yard stops being able to say that the one honest column in it is his.**
7. **The onset is the fourteenth of October 2025 and not the thirteenth; the dive was the night of the thirteenth; the order and the seizure were the morning of the fourteenth at half past six; and those are three different things and may never be fused.**
8. **The sentence's attribution: it is a shape of sentence, first recorded as Calder Marr's in a hearing room in Morrow Quay on the first of May in Volume 02 Chapter 97, no fisher in Volume 02 is recorded as having said it, and it was said SIX times in Volume 04 in Chapters 157, 169, 180, 185, 186 and 196, by four people, in four places, about five different things, and improved on by nobody. No character remarked on how often it has been said and no fisher claimed to have invented it.**
9. **The book of hours: sixteen lines over three nights is the first thing about a listening buoy and is entered, no chapter turned it into a stage, the stage was not taken, and its costs of maintenance, public exposure and dependence on a trained crew are NOT DISCHARGED. The Bellwright stage is Volume 5 and requires a crew.**
10. **The five bells and the four people of the Sill: at about five and a half fathoms inside four hundred yards, not raised, offered, argued or promised, on nobody's schedule, the four alive with no boat and no wages and about nine marks between them, their limit standing and not in any record, and the holder has not asked twice to have it lifted.**
11. **The two objects of nobody's: neither has moved, one tarpaulin still has a corner off it since the seventeenth of June, the day-count is stated once in each chapter in which it appears with one knowing exception in a two-day chapter, AND THE DAY-COUNT IS NOT MONOTONIC. The state files' monotonicity claim is withdrawn in place with the reason on its face; no chapter figure is moved; the volume's closing figure of a hundred and seventy-five days stands.**
12. **The two-calendar fault is ruled, not repaired.** The almanac is not the rule of this book, the chapters decide every date, six single-chapter date faults are repaired, and no planning file may print a day table a chapter can contradict. `outline/volume-05.md` lock L1 prints no table and lock L1a states the fault.
13. **The market wall is ruled on Chapter 155, which stages the act.** A sheet goes up on the outside of whatever is there; the sheet on that wall is the one of the twenty-fifth of August with the return under it and nothing on top of it; the two naval notices came down in August and were not taken down by this yard; and there is no sheet of the fifteenth of August on that wall because it was one of the four that came off on the sixteenth. Chapters 192 and 199 are repaired; `chapters/volume-03/chapter-0150.md:39` is flagged and not repaired.
14. **The manifest in the copy record is the *Aster Gannet*'s own**, and therefore the compartment is still not named, the three Gannet documents are still three, no step of the compartment's finding was restated in either form, the item is not a fourth item and may not become one, and a Volume 05 chapter may use the discrepancy and may not close it.
15. **The card-file precedent, verified.** `outline/batches/` holds five files. Four of the five Volume 04 batches — 0002, 0003, 0004 and 0005 — were written from `outline/volume-04.md` alone with no card file and each of those four prompts says so in its own opening; Batch 0001 was written from `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001.md`, which exists and is recorded as followed. **No series-wide tally is given here and none should be invented, because an invented tally is the same class of error as an unsummed total. NO CARD FILE WAS INVENTED FOR VOLUME 05 BATCH 0001 AND THE ONE QUEUED PROMPT SAYS SO IN ITS OWN FIRST LINE.**

### OPEN BY DESIGN

- **What an office that has kept records for three hundred years thought it was guarding against at the point at which it decided what it was allowed to lose.** Asked in a market on Wednesday the second of December in about forty people's hearing, refused in about ten seconds, and the refusal is on the page with a second bare second-column fact against it. **It may not be answered in a room, may not be answered with a document, may not be answered by naming a compact, and a bare second-column fact with no name in it may not become a document.**
- **The six hands of the seven joints, the four hundred yards, the compartment, the run under this town, the Oren mark, the first echo's witness, Neave Tarrant, the shard of worked glass nobody can read, the three places with no joint, the chart and its bell notation, the two-custody-number question, the lead plug's number, and the delayed note's answer at the Volume 15 midpoint.**
- **The harbor's poverty, which is not fixed for anything and was not fixed by anything this volume published.**

## The Volume 04 close's hand-over to Volume 05 — six open threads, in one place

**RE-DERIVED FROM THE FIFTY CHAPTERS BY THE AUDIT AND NOT COPIED FROM ANY PROMPT. SIX, AND NO SEVENTH IS OPENED BY ACCIDENT.**

1. **WHO ALTERED THE MANIFEST, AND WHAT AN OFFICE THOUGHT IT WAS GUARDING AGAINST WHEN IT DECIDED WHAT IT WAS ALLOWED TO LOSE.** The copy shows a line was altered and says on its own face that it does not show by whose hand, at whose instance or with what intent. **The manifest is the *Aster Gannet*'s own. The new-volume question was asked in a market in this harbor on Wednesday the second of December and not answered, and the reason is on the page. THE OFFICE'S OWN ACKNOWLEDGMENT OF THE TWENTY-NINTH OF NOVEMBER IS THE ONLY DOCUMENT IN THE SERIES THAT DESCRIBES WHAT THAT OFFICE THOUGHT IT WAS DOING, AND IT DESCRIBES HARM AND NOT EMERGENCY. THIS IS THE ONE THREAD THAT IS A DOCUMENT AND A QUESTION ABOUT AN INSTITUTION, AND IT IS THE SPINE OF VOLUME 05.**
2. **THE THIRD COLUMN, THE BOY, AND THE PENCIL.** Off the stone since the fifteenth of May, forty marks standing whole, the three nots standing, the head of the column keeping the boy's hand, the column empty and unfilled since the first of August, nobody appointed, and the party on the record as not knowing where he is and not asking. **A column nobody fills is a column somebody else fills, and the day a clerk with a pen fills it is the day this yard stops being able to say that the one honest column in it is his.**
3. **THE NINE-LINE MARK, WHICH CLOSES IN VOLUME 5 AND NOT BEFORE.** Not referred to, not named, not explained in any of the fifty chapters, and the nine places in six books are not the same thing and were not converted into it.
4. **THE TWO DATES IN A CITY: a copy of the said record is to be produced to the Court of Wreck Claims on the fourth day of January, and the writ runs to midnight on the first of March with the second season a named absence.** This yard has asked no office for anything in aid of the first, has sent nothing to a city, has not written to a man in a coat whose name is on no page of this book, and has not been to the counter of that circuit office since the twenty-fourth of November.
5. **THE FIVE BELLS AT ABOUT FIVE AND A HALF FATHOMS INSIDE FOUR HUNDRED YARDS, ON NOBODY'S SCHEDULE, AND THE FOUR PEOPLE WHO OWN THEM AND WHO HOLD A LIMIT THIS YARD HAS NOT ASKED TWICE TO HAVE LIFTED.** Alive, no boat, no wages, about nine marks between them, and the limit is not in any record and cannot be put in one because this yard holds a writ and a writ is a channel.
6. **MARA VENN'S FORTY MARKS, FIRST AFTER FOOD, UNPAID, AND THE LETTER OF THE TWENTY-SEVENTH OF NOVEMBER IN WHICH SHE PRICED AN ASK AND REFUSED IT AND TOLD THIS YARD NOT TO ASK AGAIN THIS WINTER** — and the answer is not a purchase, not a forgiveness and not a repair, and the yard did not tell her about the copy and is not going to.

### Carried and paid

- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE FOURTH OF JANUARY IS ON A BENCH IN A SHED AND NOBODY HAS DONE ANYTHING ABOUT IT.** Entered in the holder's own left hand in the first column of Chapter 201 with the date and the hour on it and the whole of the reason: this party has not asked any office for anything in aid of that day, has not sent to a city, has not written to a man in a coat whose name is on no page of this book, and has not been to the counter of the circuit office since the twenty-fourth of November, **because a thing done in order to be ready is a thing that arrives with a date on it.** A copy of the said record is to be produced to that court on the fourth day of January 2027 and a party which does not produce it is not fined, because a court does not fine a poor man, and the matter simply goes on. **A PARTY WHICH DOES NOT PRODUCE IT IS NOT FINED, THE COURT FINDS AGAINST NOBODY, A PERSON WHO IS NOT EXAMINED IS NOT A PERSON WHO HAS BEEN REFUSED, AND NONE OF THOSE SENTENCES WAS RESTATED, IMPROVED ON, OR USED IN ANY ARGUMENT IN THIS BATCH.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE CROWN'S POSITION ARRIVED, AND IT IS TRUE ENOUGH TO BE DANGEROUS, AND IT WAS NOT MADE FALSE.** One sentence, in a man's mouth, on the ninth of December: *testimony is safe in one place and nowhere else, and this harbor is not that one, and that is not an opinion, it is what three hundred years of an office is.* **Nobody said it was a lie. Nobody said the man was anything at all. No villain, no antagonist, no institution and no ministry was invented to carry it.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE HARBOR AUTHORITY AGREES, IN A NARROWER SENSE THAN THE MOVEMENT ASKED FOR, AND THAT IS THE STRONGER FORM.** The officer of the harbor authority said in the open air in about eleven people's hearing that a thing kept in Morrow Quay is a thing kept in Morrow Quay, and gave the reason, and named six things his office cannot do, and said he was not going to do the rest of it and pretend afterwards that it was part of it. **The price was named before he said yes and it fell on his own office, not on a working man: that office becomes a name in this business, and every party who comes up that hill about anything at all is going to be told at the foot of those steps that it has an opinion about a copy of a record, for years, including the ones who have nothing to do with this party. IT WENT ON A PAGE OF HIS OWN AND NOT IN HIS LEDGER, BECAUSE THE LEDGER IS SPENT, THE NINTH ACKNOWLEDGEMENT IS SPENT, A TENTH MAY NOT BE ENTERED AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR TWICE, AND A PAGE OF HIS OWN IS NOT A RECORD AND CANNOT BE PRODUCED ON DEMAND. HE IS STILL NOT A FRIEND OF THIS PARTY.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE BATCH'S DISCOVERY, AND IT IS THE YARD'S OWN METHOD TURNED AGAINST THE YARD.** The eleven-line list of the twenty-second of November was taken down, read aloud again, and set beside the form. **A place a copy of a record is kept in is a room, and the eleven lines are the answer to that form, and the answer to that form is eleven lines this yard cannot put in an answer** — because an answer carries the name of the party, the place and the date, and the limit of the four of the Sill is a reason, and a reason does not go into a record, and this yard holds a writ and a writ is a channel and a man with a writ is the thing that limit was written against. **The limit was read aloud once, in a shed, on the fourteenth of December, and is on the page: the account is not to be read out, is not to go into a book that leaves this harbor, is not to be said to a man with a seal, and not one name to any office in any city. NOBODY'S NAME WENT NEAR A PIECE OF PAPER ABOUT A FORM.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — A REFUSAL WAS READ AS AN INTENTION TO KEEP, AND THE GAP BETWEEN THE TWO COLUMNS WAS NOT CLOSED.** The holder said in a street that he could not say whether the form would be answered yes or no, and it was read as a man who is thinking about it, and he did not take it back and did not correct it, **because a correction is the reason and the reason is the four of the Sill and it does not go into a street.** The chapter's two columns are the reason against the line and the fact that a reason does not travel, and the entry says the gap has not been closed and is not going to be closed in December, and that in about four years a man in a city is going to have both of them and neither one of them in the other's language.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE SHAPE OF A THING NOBODY CAN CHECK WAS PRICED IN A TRADE, TWICE, AND THE TWO PRICES ARE NOT THE SAME PRICE.** A page that anybody in nine places could check is nine people who each went and got it, and nine men's time, and a man's time in a working port in December is a day at about three marks, **so the price of a checkable page is the whole of a day's work at a seam.** A page that nobody can check is a sheet and a shed and no money at all, and is the one that cannot be shown to anybody. **And a paragraph is a trade and a trade travels, and the moment a reason goes on a page it is a paragraph, and a reason cannot be kept in a paragraph.** NO PAGE OF THE COPY OF THE RECORD WAS READ ALOUD IN THAT ROOM AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM HEARD ONE LINE OF IT.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE COST, AND IT IS NOT ANY OF THE THREE COSTS ALREADY PAID IN VOLUME 04.** **A HIRED MAN'S DAY ON A LIGHTER, PAID FOR IN ADVANCE OUT OF ANOTHER MAN'S POCKET ON THE SATURDAY EVENING AND NOT GIVEN BACK ON THE MONDAY MORNING, because two men of the first lay were saying at the head of a dock that a yard up this hill is going to hold a record for the coast, like a thing that had been settled, and a man of twenty-six heard them and decided that a day's work of his might be read out in a town he has never been to, and that he has got a girl at a school in a town four days up this coast. IT IS NOT THE THIRD LAY'S FOUR DAYS AND NOT A MAN OF THE FIRST LAY'S MONDAY AND NOT A MORNING OF THE TIDE GANG, AND ITS SHAPE IS A MAN WHO WAS PAID AND DID NOT COME. THE PRICE WAS ENTERED ON THE SUNDAY BEFORE IT WAS ASKED FOR, THE YARD DID NOT ASK FOR IT, AND THE YARD IS NOT GOING TO PAY IT, AND THE REASON IS ON THE PAGE.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE TURN, IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND, IN THE FIRST COLUMN, WITH THE DATE AND THE HOUR ON IT.** **A yard which has spent four volumes saying that a record a man can check is the only kind worth having has found out in three weeks that being checkable is being findable, and that the instrument it built to make itself credible is the instrument that is going to make it locatable, and that it built that instrument on purpose on the seventh of July 2026 and nobody made it do it. IT WENT IN THE FIRST COLUMN AND NOT THE SECOND BECAUSE IT IS CHECKABLE BY ANY MAN WHO CAN GET OFF A MOLE, and it was not improved on and nobody in the shed argued with it.**
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — A COST THE YARD PAID ITSELF.** A day of about three marks refused on the twenty-fifth, the first day in four volumes that this yard has turned money down, with the figure entered against the day and the reason against the line, and the reason is a figure and not a principle.
- **CARRIED, AND PAID — THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION, REFERRED TO ONCE AND NOT ANSWERED, WITH THE SAME REFUSAL AND NO IMPROVEMENT ON IT.** A question asked in a market in this harbor on Wednesday the second of December, in about forty people's hearing, by a man of fifty-one of the first lay who is master of a coaster of about a hundred and ninety tons of his own: what an office in this country that has kept records for three hundred years thought it was guarding against at the point at which it decided what it was allowed to lose. **It is entered in the first column of Chapter 210 with its date, its asker, his age and his tonnage, and the yard's answer is the refusal already on the page, and the entry says the yard is not going to say it a second time about the same thing and is not going to call a fact an answer in a market and is not going to answer it in a book either. NO CHARACTER IN THIS BATCH ASKS THAT QUESTION. NO OFFICE ANSWERS IT. NO DOCUMENT ANSWERS IT. NO COMPACT IS NAMED, AND THE WORD *HARBORS* IS ON NO PAGE OF THIS BATCH IN ANY FORM.**

### Opened or advanced by this batch

- **NEW, AND PAID — A FORM WITH NO DATE AND NO SIGNATURE, AND THE NAME OF AN OFFICE AT THE FOOT OF IT THAT IS NOT NAMED IN THIS BOOK.** It is the form of an office in a city of the same kind as the form that acknowledged the discrepancy on the twenty-ninth of November and signed nothing. **It is not the circuit office, not the Court of Wreck Claims, not the harbor authority and not the coast office, and the coast office said no sentence in these twenty days and may not be given a third one that answers anything. The man who carried it up the hill stayed unnamed and the reason is on the page.** It proposes that this yard hold a copy of a record for the coast and be named in an entry as the person who holds it. It says the date of the answer is the only date on it. **It says nothing about what follows an answer of no. IT IS STILL ON THE BENCH IN THE SHED AT THE CLOSE OF THE BATCH AND HAS NOT BEEN ANSWERED AND HAS NOT BEEN PUT IN THE STOVE, AND A DECISION NOT TO WRITE A SENTENCE IS ENTERED IN THE FIRST COLUMN AND IT IS A DECISION AND NOT A REFUSAL TO THE OFFICE.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — AN ADDRESS, AND THE FINDING THAT BEING FINDABLE IS THE COST.** A printer's letter of the twentieth of December, dated the same day, arriving on the twenty-third, from a man of forty-four with a trade and no office. **The mechanism is narrow and it is worse than a threat: a sheet bought at a penny at a counter in this market on the sixth of December, a half sheet with the name of an office at the foot of it and no man's name anywhere on it, went up a coast road in a box of fish with a man who sells ice, a printer kept it because his trade keeps things, and on the ninth of December a man came to that counter, bought type and nothing else, and asked whether the printer could say where a copy of a record was. THE OBJECT OF THE FINDING IS THE COPY OF A RECORD IN THIS YARD'S OWN HANDS AND THE ACKNOWLEDGMENT OF THE TWENTY-NINTH OF NOVEMBER, AND THE SHEET OF THE FIFTEENTH OF AUGUST IS NOT ON ANY PAGE OF THIS BATCH AND ROWAN TREWIN'S ARGUMENT IS NOT MADE AGAIN. THE YARD'S OWN METHOD IS THE INSTRUMENT: an acknowledgment with an office's name at the foot of it and no signature on it is the one place in this country where a man in a city can look to find out where this yard is, and this yard did not write it and did not pay for it and did not ask for it and did not allow it.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — TWO FIGURES THAT CAME OUT OF A TRADE IN THIS HARBOR, IN THE YARD'S OWN HAND, AND NEITHER OF THEM WAS PAID FOR.** Nine pence is the paper in a checkable page and nine men's time is the rest of it; a sheet and a shed and no money at all is an uncheckable one. **A penny went into a copyist's own hand on the eighteenth for a sheet of her paper, and it was for this yard's own use and not for a copy of anything, and the reason against the line is that this party cannot put a price in a record out of a person's mouth.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A PRINTED SHEET'S PATH IS ON THE PAGE AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME A SHEET HAS MOVED IN THIS BOOK.** Fish box, coast road, a printer's hand-list with a day, a price and a buyer's name, a man buying type, a town written down, and a gap where an address should be. **The people in it are ordinary people in their own trades: a man who sells ice, a printer, a man buying type. NONE OF THEM IS A VILLAIN AND NONE IS ON A LADDER.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — A MAN WHO SAID HE WAS NOT THE MAN WHO DIRECTS IT, AND THAT HE DID NOT KNOW WHAT PICKING UP A SHEET OFF A BENCH IS, AND THAT THE OFFICE THAT MADE THE SHEET DOES NOT SAY EITHER.** He asked and was told the office does not decide it. He came up a hill on foot in a coat that had been in weather and went down it and nobody followed him.
- **NEW, AND PAID — A HIRED MAN'S DECISION, AND IT IS THE BATCH'S ONLY CONSEQUENCE IN A BODY THAT IS NOT A WORKING CREW MEMBER.** A man of twenty-six gave up a day of his own work that he had been paid for in advance, over a sentence said in a street, on the ground that a day's work of his might be read out in a town he has never been to. **He is not named and was not asked about and his name is not in this yard's book and a man of fifty-five refused to give it and said that if this party went down to that quay he would be the man who told it.**
- **NEW, AND PAID — THE HARBOR'S POVERTY AT THE END OF MOVEMENT 1, AND IT IS WORSE.** Seven marks, six shillings and four pence in a bag against four marks and six shillings and five pence twenty days earlier, **the whole of the difference being one day at a seam and a sheet of paper at a penny, and a day of about three marks refused by this yard itself**; **three wages of four marks not paid and nine weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them**; a hired man's day gone on a lighter; the north berth out; the mole-head light not on because a light costs oil; the tide gang four men short; the fire party's hose dead; one herring berth of two out; the hard at Ashtide unanswered; five marks of compressor's gland unpaid and useless and the thing after the gland is the hose and this harbor has no hose; nineteen winters in a list in a city; the *Sea Wren* at the pile with this party not having thanked him; **and the cistern charged at no figure on six rota days in twenty days and walked on none, and the charge of the ninth of November standing entered with no figure against it, and the party on the record as not going to say what six charges and that one make between them.**

### Ruled

- **RULED — NO POWER STAGE OF ANY KIND WAS TAKEN AND NONE MAY BE.** The Faultreader stage is spent, is not re-taken, is not improved, and **the word is on no page of these ten chapters.** **No person of this yard was in any water on any day of this batch, no finding of worked glass was made, nothing was raised, and no return of any kind was made in twenty days.** The book of hours stands at sixteen lines, was not extended, and its costs of maintenance, public exposure and dependence on a trained crew are NOT discharged. **The word *seam* on a page of this batch is the mole's own first, second, third and fifth seams and a day of work at a seam, and is not the perception once.**
- **RULED — THE CONDITION IS NAMED NOWHERE IN THIS BATCH.** The body is charged and the name is not written. **The four lines above the named line of the twentieth of July 2026 are still four, are still nameless, are not struck out, and a request to strike a line out of a column is refused in the same words it is refused for a line in any column.**
- **RULED — THE FORM OF THE COURT OF WRECK CLAIMS WAS NOT PRODUCED AGAIN, READ OUT AGAIN, OR USED IN ANY ARGUMENT.** It is on the bench in the shed and it says what it says. **The Second Bell eligibility is gone and is not restored, is not applied for, and is not the writ. NOTHING IN THIS BATCH SAYS THE COURT REFUSED HIM ANYTHING OR FOUND AGAINST HIM, AND NOTHING SAYS HE HAS SAT THE EXAMINATION SINCE SEPTEMBER 2025. The writ runs to midnight on the first of March and the two dates have not been joined and were not joined in three weeks.**
- **RULED — NO NAME OF A PERSON, AN OFFICE THAT DECIDED ANYTHING, OR A HAND WAS GIVEN FOR THE MANIFEST.** The copy of the record was carried to a counter in Chapter 207 and was not read aloud, and its discrepancy heads were not restated, and the discrepancy was neither used nor closed. **The compartment is not named, the three Gannet documents are still three, no step of the finding is restated, the item is not a fourth item, no digit of any of the three numbers is on any page of this batch, and the question no paper in this harbor or in that office's copying room answers was not put to anybody in three weeks.**
- **RULED — THE QUIETENING, THE CROWN SEAL, THE OFFICE OF RETURNING MEMORY, ORISON AND ALL NINE FINAL ROUTES ARE NAMED NOWHERE IN THIS BATCH, AND THE WORD *HARBORS* IS ON NO PAGE OF IT IN ANY FORM.** A case-insensitive search of the ten chapters for every one of those, and for *Lowfen*, *Ternwatch*, *Saltmarch*, *Tidewatch*, *Cinder*, *Pale Shoals*, *Morrow Line* and *Ashtide Run*, returns nothing. **THE NAME OF THE COMPACT IS MOVEMENT 3'S AND MAY NOT BE USED BEFORE IT, AND NO COMPACT IS NAMED AS THE ANSWER TO ANYTHING IN THIS BATCH.**
- **RULED — NO NEW ANTAGONIST, NO NEW INSTITUTION, NO NEW FINAL ENEMY, AND NO NEW CHARACTER WITH A TITLE, A UNIFORM, A COMMISSION OR A MOTIVE OUTSIDE HER OWN TRADE'S WORDS.** The people who appear are Denzil Hare, who was in Volume 04; a man of the second lay with a lighter, who is a man with a lighter; a man of the second lay with a boat of about seventy tons; and Jonas Peake, a printer, who gives his trade and says the trade is not an office. **Captain Veyra Holt is not in this harbor and is not in a room and her name is on no page. Halven Rook is not in a room. Magister Ilya Serr is on no page of this batch at all.**
- **RULED — THE BELL, THE BELLWRIGHT AND THE LEAD PLUG ARE UNTOUCHED.** No boat went off the south end, no second eight marks was entered as paid, the bellwright was not asked in a shed, he is not in this batch as a person, no digit of anything he said on the second of July is printed, and the chest was not opened and was not brought down the hill. **The standing sentence said in a street on the third of May is quoted in Chapter 210's closing position in its own terms and stands unreversed.**
- **RULED — THE PLATE AT THE FIFTH SEAM WAS NOT REACHED, NOT MEASURED, NOT TOUCHED AND NOT ASKED ABOUT, AND NO BETTER LETTER WAS WRITTEN ABOUT IT IN THREE WEEKS, AND THE NOTE STANDS OPEN.** The only mechanism anybody has named for reaching it is a change of politics in an office.
- **RULED — THE THIRD COLUMN WAS NOT FILLED ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH AND NOBODY WAS APPOINTED TO IT, AND THE BOY'S HAND IS STILL AT THE HEAD OF IT.** The three nots of the eighth of June stand and are entered. Forty marks stands whole. The boy is in no chapter of this batch as a person. **The name of the man of Ashtide is on no page of this batch, he was not asked anything, nobody was sent for him, and nobody in this batch said a word about him.**
- **RULED — THE CHANDLER'S NOTE IS NOT RE-WRITEN, ITS THREE COUNTS OF WEEKS STAND UNMOVED AT TWENTY-TWO, THIRTEEN AND ELEVEN, AND THE YARD DID NOT SAY IT OUT LOUD A FOURTH TIME AND DID NOT SAY IT ONCE IN THESE TWENTY DAYS.**
- **RULED — THE SLATE WALL'S COUNTER DID NOT RESTART AND IS THIRTY-ONE ON THE SEVENTH, THIRTY-TWO ON THE FOURTEENTH AND THIRTY-THIRD ON THE TWENTY-FIRST OF DECEMBER, ALL BARE, ALL WITH THE CLERK KEEPING HIS OWN PAGE, AND NO SITTING HELD ON ANY OF THEM.** **THE TWENTY-EIGHTH IS THE THIRTY-FOURTH AND IS OUTSIDE THIS BATCH.**
- **RULED — THE MARKET OFFICE WAS NOT USED ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH.** It is open on a Tuesday and a Friday morning, and this batch's days are the sixth to the twenty-fifth. **THE HEARING ROOM WAS NOT USED ON ANY DAY OF THIS BATCH AND THE MARKET WALL WAS NOT TOUCHED ON ANY DAY OF IT, and no sheet went up on any wall in this harbor in the twenty days and none came off, and the two naval notices are not on that wall and were not taken down by this yard. A SHEET THAT GOES UP GOES UP ON THE OUTSIDE OF WHATEVER IS THERE, AND THE YARD'S OWN FOURTH RETURN IS STILL ON THAT WALL UNDER ONE SHEET.**
- **RULED — THE READ-OUT DEVICE WAS USED ONCE IN THIS BATCH**, at the head of this dock on the afternoon of Thursday the twenty-fourth, in about eleven people's hearing, in about nine minutes, and not one line of the second column was read out in it. **THE READ-BACK IS IN SIX OF THE TEN CHAPTERS AND NOT FOUR, IN THREE FORMS, AND THE HOUSE'S OWN STRING TEST FOR *read it back* OR *read back after her* RETURNS THE SAME SIX: the shed-evening form in 201, 205, 208 and 210; the same room and the same hour on the sheet in 202, which the first record of this batch left out of its count; and the board's own device at about half past one on the mole in 204, which is a working day's device and not the shed's.** Chapter 206's read-back is deliberately absent and the chapter says why.
- **RULED — THE SHAPE OF SENTENCE WAS SAID ONCE IN THIS BATCH, in Chapter 209, by the ship's hand, about a copy of a record in this yard's hands and this yard's name on a line in a printer's book.** No character remarked on how often it has been said, none said it belonged to a Volume 02 room, and no fisher claimed to have invented it.
- **RULED — THE GRID.** This batch takes its days from `outline/volume-05.md` lock L1 as corrected and from the chapters before it, **on the grid of the first of December 2026 being a Tuesday, which is the grid of Chapters 171 to 200 and not the older one.** Twenty consecutive days, two to a chapter, no day missing and no day in two chapters. **NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH STATES A YEAR. The only backward references with a weekday on them are the six in the standing list of unpaid wages, which carry the days the chapters that staged them gave them. The two-calendar fault this volume inherits is not noticed anywhere in these ten chapters and no character in them explains a calendar to another character.**
- **RULED — THE DUPLICATION AUDIT WAS RUN IN THREE PASSES OVER THE TEN CHAPTERS AND OVER ALL FIFTY CHAPTERS OF VOLUME 04 TOGETHER, AND THE RESULT IS: ZERO byte-identical duplicate paragraphs of twelve words or more (three were found and three were repaired in place in this batch, first instance left, second varied); FOUR forty-word opening matches, all four ruled and none repaired; ZERO proper containment pairs; and every repeated sentence of eight words or more is the standing inventory, a figure, or a document's printed text.** **THE THREE PASSES WERE RUN AGAIN AFTER THIS BATCH'S REVIEW REPAIR AND THE RESULT IS THE SAME, AND THE AUDIT'S OWN COORDINATES, WHICH DID NOT RESOLVE IN THE FIRST RECORD, ARE NOW VERIFIED: `chapter-0206.md:41` and `chapter-0210.md:7` for the cistern paragraphs, `chapter-0210.md:37` against `chapter-0200.md:77` for the read-out's last line, and `chapter-0205.md:35` and `chapter-0205.md:37` for the varied limit pair.** The detail is `state/continuity.md` § 10 of the Batch 0001 section.

### Open by design

- **OPEN BY DESIGN — what an office that has kept records for three hundred years thought it was guarding against at the point at which it decided what it was allowed to lose.** **Referred to once in this batch, in a first column, with its date, its asker and the yard's own refusal, and NOT answered, and not improved on, and no character asked it. It may not be answered in a room, may not be answered with a document, may not be answered by naming a compact, and a bare second-column fact with no name in it may not become a document. The office's three hundred years is the office's; the communities' practice is six centuries; the two figures are not interchangeable in any sentence.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — who altered the manifest.** Carried. The copy says it does not show and names no person. It may not be shown in this volume and no chapter of this batch guessed at it, and the copy was carried to a counter in Chapter 207 and not read aloud.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the answer inside the delayed note.** Eleven logged occasions and two with no call in them, **neither advanced in this batch by arithmetic or by a measurement, no occasion entered, no measurement taken, nothing written down with a date against it**, the delay a hand's width of a second and unmoved, and nobody in this harbor has ever said why. **The answer is reserved to the Volume 15 midpoint.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the plate at the fifth seam and its note, CARRIED OPEN AND UNCHANGED.** The document that would close it was produced three times in Volume 04 and a document produced once in this series is spent.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the five bells and the four people of the Sill.** At about five and a half fathoms inside four hundred yards, not raised, not offered, not argued, not promised, on nobody's schedule, the four alive with no boat and no wages and about nine marks between them, their limit standing and not in any record, and **the holder did not ask to have it lifted once in twenty days and did not put the limit near a piece of paper about a form in a city.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the third column, the boy, and the pencil.** Empty, unfilled since the first of August, nobody appointed, and the boy in no chapter of this batch as a person.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the nine-line mark**, which closes in Volume 5 and not before, and which is referred to nowhere in this batch and named nowhere in it.
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the custody items and the live question no paper in this harbor answers, and the lead plug's number, and the run under this town, and the compartment, and the first echo's witness, and Neave Tarrant, nine winters, not found, one line, and this yard has not gone, and the three places with no joint, and the shard of worked glass nobody can read, and the chart and its bell notation in a vessel's keeping, and the page that went out of a file and the two words, both spent and neither to be spent again.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the coast road.** In type in a city beside a pilot's figure, neither withdrawn, **not resolved and not called anything**, the Kettle not looked at, the refused licence not re-decided, and **the eleventh line of the list of what is not going into a room not moved off that list in three weeks and not going to be moved in a December.**
- **OPEN BY DESIGN — the harbor's poverty, which is not fixed for anything and was not fixed by anything this yard did in three weeks.**

### What Movement 2 inherits, in one place

1. **THE MOVEMENT'S TURN IS PAID AND IT IS A STANDING POSITION, NOT AN EVENT: being checkable is being findable, and the yard's own instrument is the instrument that will make it locatable, and that is entered in the first column in the holder's own left hand with the date and the hour on it and was not improved on.**
2. **FOUR PIECES OF PAPER ARE IN THIS YARD'S HANDS AND A FIFTH IS IN A DRAWER, AND NEITHER OF THE TWO QUESTIONS IN THEM HAS BEEN ANSWERED.** The copy of a record of the twenty-fourth of November; the acknowledgment of the twenty-ninth; the form of the Court of Wreck Claims of the fourth of December with the fourth of January on it; the form of an unnamed office in a city with no signature and no date on it, still on the bench under a pump; and a printer's letter of the twentieth of December with one question in it. **THE ANSWER TO THE FORM IS NOT WRITTEN AND THE ANSWER TO THE PRINTER IS NOT WRITTEN, AND BOTH OF THOSE ARE ENTERED IN THE FIRST COLUMN. A DECISION NOT TO WRITE A SENTENCE IS A DECISION AND NOT A REFUSAL TO THE OFFICE.**
3. **THE CROWN'S POSITION IS ON THE PAGE IN ONE SENTENCE AND IS TRUE ENOUGH TO BE DANGEROUS AND WAS NOT MADE FALSE, and it has not been answered, and nobody in this harbor has said it is a lie.**
4. **THE HARBOR AUTHORITY AGREES, IN A NARROW SENSE, AND HAS PAID FOR IT WITH ITS OWN FINDABILITY, and its page is a page of his own and not a record, and it cannot be produced on demand.** It has entered nothing in a ledger, the ninth acknowledgement is spent and a tenth may not be entered, and it is not a friend of this party.
5. **THE ELEVEN-LINE LIST HAS BEEN TESTED AGAINST AN OFFER AND THE DISAGREEMENT IS THE BATCH'S DISCOVERY, and the disagreement is that the list is a list of what does not go into a room and the answer cannot be written down.** A later batch may not resolve that disagreement on the page.
6. **THE STANDING FIGURES, all checkable in pence: seven marks, six shillings and four pence in the bag; twenty-six lines and one hundred and four marks in the wage column with the fifth of March the oldest; twenty envelopes on a bench; NINE weeks fallen due with no envelope standing against them, the fifteenth of August, the fifth of September, the sixth of October, the third of November, the seventeenth of November, the first of December and the eighth, fifteenth and twenty-second of December instant.** The three Tuesdays of this batch were the eighth, the fifteenth and the twenty-second and the wage was not paid on any of them.
7. **THE THIRTY-FIRST, THIRTY-SECOND AND THIRTY-THIRD SLATE-WALL WIPES ARE GONE AND ALL THREE WERE BARE, and the twenty-eighth of December is the thirty-fourth. THE NEXT BATCH'S FIRST MONDAY IS THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF DECEMBER AND ITS WAGE TUESDAY IS THE TWENTY-NINTH, AND THE MONTH ENDS ON A TUESDAY.**
8. **THE FAR BENCH WAS OCCUPIED ON FOUR DAYS, THE SEVENTH, THE ELEVENTH, THE EIGHTEENTH AND THE TWENTY-FIRST OF DECEMBER 2026, AND ON NO OTHER DAY OF THE TWENTY, AND NEITHER BROTHER WENT OVER TO THE OTHER ON ANY OF THE FOUR. THE FIGURE OF FOUR IS COUNTED FROM THE PROSE. THE FIRST RECORD OF THIS BATCH SAID THREE AND LEFT OUT THE ELEVENTH, WHICH WAS STAGED IN CHAPTER 203'S PROSE AND NOT ENTERED IN THAT CHAPTER'S FIRST COLUMN; THE ELEVENTH IS NOW IN THAT FIRST COLUMN AND A BATCH 2 WRITER MAY COUNT FOUR OFF THE CHAPTERS AND OFF THE STATE FILES ALIKE. IT WAS ALSO EMPTY ON THE SIXTEENTH, THE SEVENTEENTH, THE TWENTY-FOURTH AND THE TWENTY-FIFTH AT ANY HOUR, AND THE LINE IS ENTERED WITH NO FIGURE OF WHAT CAME BEFORE IT: A MAN WHO COMES UP AND SEES THE BENCH EMPTY AND ASKS WHAT IT WAS THE DAY BEFORE IS TOLD THAT THEY DO NOT KNOW. THE SEPTEMBER CLAIM IS WITHDRAWN.**
9. **THE TWO COSTS ALREADY PAID IN MOVEMENT 1 THAT MAY NOT BE PAID AGAIN: a hired man's day on a lighter, entered before it was asked for and not paid; and a day of about three marks refused by this yard itself, entered with the figure against the day. AND THE THIRD, WHICH IS NOT A FIGURE AND IS THE ONE A BATCH 2 WRITER IS MOST LIKELY TO WALK INTO: TWO MEN OF THE FIRST LAY WERE SAYING A THING ABOUT THIS YARD AT THE HEAD OF THAT DOCK ON THURSDAY THE SEVENTEENTH AND AGAIN ON SATURDAY THE NINETEENTH, AND THE YARD WENT UP ON NEITHER DAY, AND THE REASON AGAINST BOTH LINES IS THAT A CORRECTION IS THE REASON AND THE REASON DOES NOT GO INTO A STREET. A MOVEMENT 2 CHAPTER MAY PUT THOSE TWO MEN BACK IN THAT STREET, may not have this yard go up to either of them, may not name either of them, and may not make a third occasion of it out of nothing. THE READ-OUT OF MOVEMENT 1 ITEMISED BOTH DAYS, AND SO MUST ANY LATER ACCOUNTING.**
10. **THE NEW-VOLUME QUESTION IS OPEN AND IS REFERRED TO ONCE IN THIS BATCH AND WAS NOT ANSWERED, AND IT IS THE SPINE OF THE VOLUME. The court of Wreck Claims wants a copy of the record on the fourth of January and this yard has asked no office for anything in aid of it, has sent nothing to a city, has not written to a man in a coat whose name is on no page of this book, and has not been to the counter of that circuit office since the twenty-fourth of November 2026.**
11. **THE SIX OPEN THREADS OF THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE ARE INHERITED AND THIS BATCH OPENED NO SEVENTH. The new items listed under *Opened or advanced by this batch* are the volume's own planned work in `outline/volume-05.md` § *Movement 1* and are not a hand-over. NO CHAIN OF CUSTODY, NO PAGE IN A DRAWER IN FIVE TOWNS, AND NO AGREEMENT ABOUT WHO MAY READ A PAGE IS DESCRIBED IN THIS BATCH, BECAUSE THAT PRACTICE IS NEW AND IS MOVEMENT 5'S, AND NO LATER BATCH MAY AGE IT OR DESCRIBE IT BEFORE MOVEMENT 5 PUTS IT IN.**
